This article is about the character Naruto Uzumaki. For the similarly named manga and anime series, see [Naruto (series)](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_\(series\) "Naruto (series)"). For other uses, see [Naruto (disambiguation)](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_\(disambiguation\) "Naruto (disambiguation)").

**Naruto Uzumaki** (うずまきナルト, *Uzumaki Naruto*) is a [shinobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shinobi "Shinobi") of [Konohagakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konohagakure "Konohagakure") 's [Uzumaki clan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Uzumaki_clan "Uzumaki clan") and a [reincarnation](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Reincarnation "Reincarnation") of [Asura Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Asura_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Asura Ōtsutsuki"). He became the [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki") of the [Nine-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails "Nine-Tails") on the [day of his birth](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tailed_Demon_Fox%27s_Attack "Nine-Tailed Demon Fox's Attack") — a fate that caused him to be shunned by most of Konoha throughout his childhood. After joining [Team Kakashi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Kakashi "Team Kakashi"), Naruto worked hard to gain the village's acknowledgement all the while chasing his dream to become [Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage "Hokage"). In the following years, through many hardships and ordeals, he became a capable ninja, regarded as a hero both by the villagers, and soon after, the rest of the world, becoming known as the **Hero of the Hidden Leaf** (木ノ葉隠れの英雄, *Konohagakure no Eiyū*, *literally meaning:* Hero of the Hidden Tree Leaves). He soon proved to be one of the main factors in winning the [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"), leading him to achieve his dream and become the village's **Seventh Hokage** (七代目火影, *Nanadaime Hokage*, *literally meaning:* Seventh Fire Shadow).

## Background

![Naruto, newborn with his mother Kushina.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/e5/Naruto_and_Kushina2.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20160129152021)

Naruto, newborn with his mother Kushina.

Naruto was born on the night of October 10th to [Minato Namikaze](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato_Namikaze "Minato Namikaze") (the Fourth Hokage) and [Kushina Uzumaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kushina_Uzumaki "Kushina Uzumaki") (the second jinchūriki of the Nine-Tails). He was named after [Naruto Musasabi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Musasabi "Naruto Musasabi"), the protagonist of [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya") 's [first book](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Tale_of_the_Utterly_Gutsy_Shinobi "The Tale of the Utterly Gutsy Shinobi"), which made the [Sannin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sannin "Sannin") his godfather.[^16] The [Third Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Third_Hokage "Third Hokage") made special arrangements for Minato to preserve Kushina's seal containing the Nine-Tails while she gave birth to Naruto in a remote location, assisted by midwives and [Anbu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Anbu "Anbu"). A masked man, [Tobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tobi "Tobi"), tracked down their location, however, killed the midwives and Anbu, and captured Naruto, forcing Minato to quickly rescue him and [teleport](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Flying_Thunder_God_Technique "Flying Thunder God Technique") him to a [safe house](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato%27s_Safe_House "Minato's Safe House").

|  | ![Minato and Kushina protect Naruto from the Nine-Tails.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/b0/Minato_and_Kushina_protect_Naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240124003940)  Minato and Kushina protect Naruto from the Nine-Tails. |
| --- | --- |

With Minato gone, Tobi captured Kushina and [released](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tail_Releasing_Method "Tail Releasing Method") the Nine-Tails from her, using it to [devastate Konoha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tailed_Demon_Fox%27s_Attack "Nine-Tailed Demon Fox's Attack").[^17] Minato saved Kushina and left Naruto in her care before he went to protect the village,[^18] eventually defeating Tobi and [freeing](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Contract_Seal "Contract Seal") the Nine-Tails from Tobi's control. Returning to Naruto and Kushina's location, Minato realised the only way to stop the Nine-Tails was to seal it within Naruto, believing that his son would someday need the fox's power to defeat Tobi when he returned.[^19] Since the Nine-Tails' [chakra](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra "Chakra") was too immense to be sealed into an infant, Minato [sacrificed his soul](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Dead_Demon_Consuming_Seal "Dead Demon Consuming Seal") to split the fox's chakra in half, sealing the [Yin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yin "Yin") half within himself and the [Yang](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yang "Yang") half [within](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Trigrams_Sealing_Style "Eight Trigrams Sealing Style") Naruto. After telling Naruto how much they loved him, Minato and Kushina succumbed to their wounds from protecting their son from the Nine-Tails and passed away.

Orphaned, and not having anyone else to provide for him, Naruto received monthly income from the village in order to afford daily necessities. Naruto grew up not knowing who his parents were, receiving only his mother's surname, as Hiruzen wanted to protect Naruto from his father's enemies.[^20] Minato's dying wish that Naruto be regarded as a hero was honoured by the very few who could put aside their pain and losses caused by the disaster, while the majority of Konoha, however having no knowledge of the circumstances surrounding his birth, openly ostracised and resented Naruto for containing the beast that devastated the village and took many lives; some even viewed Naruto as the Nine-Tails itself. Soon, the Third Hokage forbade anyone from mentioning the Nine-Tails, hoping that the younger generation would not blindly hate Naruto as their parents did.[^21] However, Naruto's peers emulated their parents' hatred of him, despite not knowing why. This social isolation caused Naruto to crave acknowledgement, which he would gain by pulling pranks.

![Naruto meets Hinata.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/c4/Naruto%27s_first_encounter_with_Hinata.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150711120110)

Naruto meets Hinata.

Naruto first met [Hinata Hyūga](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hinata_Hy%C5%ABga "Hinata Hyūga") on the day of her enrolment in the [Ninja Academy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ninja_Academy "Ninja Academy"),[^22] while she was being picked on by three bullies. Despite not knowing her, Naruto immediately came to her defence, but he was outnumbered and knocked unconscious, and the bullies damaged his red scarf. When Naruto awoke, Hinata thanked him for helping her and returned his scarf to him, but he let her keep it. He was unaware that the girl's growing affection for him began at that moment.[^23]

|  | ![Naruto and Sasuke in the Academy.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/c2/Young_sasuke_and_Naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210925220045)  Naruto and Sasuke in the Academy. |
| --- | --- |

In the Academy, Naruto became a student of [Iruka Umino](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Iruka_Umino "Iruka Umino"), who acted as a surrogate older brother to keep him in line and help him work harder. Naruto also met his classmate [Sasuke Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Uchiha "Sasuke Uchiha") and tried to befriend him, since Sasuke was alone as well. Jealous of Sasuke's skills and popularity, however,[^24] he developed a one-sided rivalry in his pursuit to prove himself just as good as, if not better than, Sasuke, wishing that someday, Sasuke would accept him as an equal.[^25] Naruto also grew close with the owner of [Ramen Ichiraku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ramen_Ichiraku "Ramen Ichiraku"), [Teuchi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Teuchi "Teuchi") and his daughter [Ayame](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ayame "Ayame"), being welcomed as their favourite customer.

## Personality

![Naruto's ninja registration photo.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/1/1e/Naruto%27s_Ninja_Registration_Photo.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150405165827)

Naruto's ninja registration photo.

Naruto is noted as boisterous, exuberant, and unorthodox, quite similar to [Hashirama Senju](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hashirama_Senju "Hashirama Senju").[^26] He inherited his mother's verbal tic, as he ends his sentences with " [Dattebayo!](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Dattebayo_and_Believe_It "Dattebayo and Believe It")" (だってばよ!) when emotional. Though he responds best to competition and is not afraid to ask for assistance, Naruto is relatively naïve and slow to understand principles or situations. He often requires an oversimplified analogy in order to grasp explanations [^27] and can even prove forgetful.[^28] Naruto is aware of his faults and admits he acts strong to mask his embarrassment and frustration about them.[^29] [^30] Despite his naivety, Naruto can be quite observant, picking up on things others miss and retaining information casually gathered through conversation.[^31]

Growing up as an orphan who suffered years of hatred and social isolation has influenced Naruto's character in a number of ways: he is heedless to formality and social standings, addressing [certain](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rock_Lee "Rock Lee") [people](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya") with nicknames instead of [honourifics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Honorific "wikipedia:Honorific"), although by the time of his adulthood, Naruto has slightly outgrown this trait.[^32] [^33] He has picky eating habits of ramen, his favourite food; he has a perverted side that manifests as attempts to peep into women's baths, although these moments are rare.[^34] Despite his quirks and the criticism he receives for them (to the point of being labelled the "ultimate problem child"), Naruto is said to have a personality that draws people to him,[^35] inspiring friendship and loyalty through acts of genuine kindness that could change a person's world view and thus built [meaningful relationships](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Uzumaki%27s_Relationships "Naruto Uzumaki's Relationships") that he lacked in early life; the [Sage of Six Paths](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_of_Six_Paths "Sage of Six Paths") believes Naruto's kindness is a special gift that allows him to save [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama") from its hatred.[^36] Naruto deeply cherishes these bonds and will go to great lengths to protect them, best seen with Sasuke after his defection from Konoha.

Another of Naruto's prominent traits is his desire for acknowledgement; his pranks around the village were for people to notice him, even if it meant by scolding him. Therefore, he dreamed of one day becoming Konoha's Hokage, the epitome of acknowledgement and respect from everyone in the village.[^37] Knowing his dream is a long, arduous and seemingly impossible path, Naruto developed his own [nindō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nind%C5%8D "Nindō") of never going back on his word. This life-long philosophy serves as the cornerstone of Naruto's great self-confidence; he believes he can accomplish any goal with enough hard work and perseverance, no matter what. This trait guides him in many aspects of life, such as his promise to bring Sasuke back to Konoha. Over time, his goal to be Hokage grew from a desire for the acknowledgement of others to a desire to help and protect the people close to him, which [Tobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tobi "Tobi") attributed to the [Will of Fire](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Will_of_Fire "Will of Fire").[^38] His nindō has since been reinforced by his understanding that shinobi were meant to endure through hardships.[^39] His belief in hard work and perseverance made him detest shortcuts to achieving, as he disqualified his son from the [Chūnin Exams](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%ABnin_Exams "Chūnin Exams") for using the banned [Kote](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kote "Kote"), and expressed disgust at [Momoshiki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Momoshiki "Momoshiki") and [Kinshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kinshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Kinshiki Ōtsutsuki") for their reliance on harvesting from [Chakra Fruits](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_Fruit "Chakra Fruit") to lazily gain power.[^40]

After the death of his master [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya"), talking to his father [Minato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato "Minato") and listening to [Nagato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nagato "Nagato") 's tragic story, Naruto grew to understand the destructive cycle of hatred and he vowed to break it in order to bring true peace to the world.[^41] To that end, his participation in the [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War") is not only to protect his friends but also to bear and erase the world's hatred all by himself. After encountering a [reincarnated](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Summoning:_Impure_World_Reincarnation "Summoning: Impure World Reincarnation") [Itachi Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Itachi_Uchiha "Itachi Uchiha"), however, Naruto realises that he cannot handle everything alone and that he must allow his friends to support and fight alongside him. As he does so in war's climax, his fighting spirit inspires the [Allied Shinobi Forces](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Allied_Shinobi_Forces "Allied Shinobi Forces") to join him and keep fighting to end the war as well, ultimately becoming an example for many people to admire, follow, and believe in.[^42] [^43]

![Naruto becomes shy around Hinata.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/3/36/Naruto_watching_Hinata_knit.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150821103014)

Naruto becomes shy around Hinata.

Naruto first met [Hinata Hyūga](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hinata_Hy%C5%ABga "Hinata Hyūga") when they were children, where his open kindness and courageous spirit earned him her admiration, which grew into love. On his part, Naruto remained mostly oblivious to Hinata's feelings for him and even considered her "weird" because of her shyness around him and his socially ostracised childhood. However, that change during the Chūnin Exams in Part I when they began to interact more and support each other, causing Naruto to like her as a friend.[^44] In Part II, as Hinata becomes willing to risk her life to protect Naruto, he, in turn, sees her as an actively strong person despite his speechlessness at her love confession. Naruto grew to love Hinata subconsciously. Still, he was unaware of it because he kept "fighting" for Sakura, which was just another way for him to compete with Sasuke. During *[The Last: Naruto the Movie](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Last:_Naruto_the_Movie "The Last: Naruto the Movie")*, Naruto finally recognises the love that Hinata has always had for him, causing him to become more bashful and protective of her, just as she was for him. Realising that she alone has ever had faith in him and that she has always been there for him, he reciprocates her feelings and desires to be with her for the rest of his life. This new-found desire made him willing to make healthier lifestyle choices such as eating vegetables and reeling in his impulsiveness, something others have had little success with doing.

As an adult, Naruto becomes wiser and more mature, though he retains some of his negligence for honourifics.[^45] Although he finally achieves his dream of becoming Hokage, its many tasks would often overwhelm Naruto to the point of him being unable to spend time with his family. He admits that part of this stems was from not having grown up with a father himself, making him unsure of how to interact appropriately with his children.[^46] Naruto also tends to be more responsible in his duties as Hokage than as a father. Nevertheless, Naruto profoundly loves his family, as he creates shadow clones to tend to them while he's busy, protects them in times of danger, spends time with them when he can, and expresses pride in his children's successes.[^40] He adopts Hiruzen's philosophy that everyone in the village is his family, believing that genuine relationships are made up of love rather than blood relations.[^47] His refusal to give his son any special treatment due to their blood-relationships, along with the insistence that the boy professionally addresses him as "Seventh" or "Hokage" while in the office, is a testament of Naruto's lack of bias and prejudice.[^40] Likewise, even in the face of people close to him committing horrible deeds, Naruto remains loyal to them, pushing aside his grief enough to see the nature of what they did.[^48] However, after facing [Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Momoshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki") and realising how his overly-committed nature serving the entire village was significantly affecting his own family's happiness, Naruto came to accept that he needed to give them more attention. As such, by allowing more help from others, Naruto was able to enjoy more time with his family, restoring the same healthy relationship he had with them before he became Hokage.

### Dark Naruto

!["Dark Naruto" reveals himself in the waterfall.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d4/Dark_Naruto_appears.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20231125040012)

"Dark Naruto" reveals himself in the waterfall.

Although he struggled to overcome the hatred of people and the shinobi world, seeking to achieve the peace that his master Jiraiya dreamed of so much, Naruto was also not free from his own hatred. When Naruto understands that it is necessary to definitively master the power of the Nine-Tails, he goes to the [Island Turtle](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genbu "Genbu") and discovers the [Falls of Truth](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Falls_of_Truth "Falls of Truth") as part of the process. There, Naruto encounters his darker half: **Dark Naruto** (闇ナルト, *Yami Naruto*).[^49] This was the personification of all of Naruto's negative feelings, such as the grudge he secretly still held against the villagers of Konoha who had always rejected him since childhood. Dark Naruto was the Nine-Tails' favourite aspect of Naruto, claiming him to be the "real" Naruto. No matter how much he resisted, the Nine-Tails would tap into the hatred that existed hidden in the depths of his heart.[^50] Naruto's conclusion to dealing with his inner hatred was not to ignore him, but rather accept his existence, understanding that this same grudge was what made him get to where he was and value his entire journey up until that moment.[^51] This whole process served as basis for Naruto to shape who he truly wanted to be from then on.

## Appearance

According to [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya"), Naruto strongly resembles his [father](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato_Namikaze "Minato Namikaze"):[^52] he has yellow-blond, spiky hair and blue eyes, while inheriting the shape of his mother's eyes and face.[^53] His trademark characteristics are the three whisker markings on his cheeks. During the [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"), [Dan Katō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Dan_Kat%C5%8D "Dan Katō") initially mistook Naruto for [Nawaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nawaki "Nawaki") due to their stark resemblance.[^54] Naruto was rather short for his age during Part I,[^55] though he grew to be taller than [Sakura](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sakura "Sakura") in Part II.[^56] Naruto originally wore green goggles on his forehead, though he discards them upon becoming a genin.

- ![Naruto in Part II (left) and in Part I (right) respectively.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/cd/Naruto_both_parts.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/118?cb=20150825074135)
	Naruto in Part II (left) and in Part I (right) respectively.

1/4

In Part I, Naruto wore an orange and blue jacket with a white collar, a white swirl with a tassel on the left side, and a red Uzumaki crest on the back. He also wore orange pants with a shuriken holster on his right knee, blue sandals, and a blue [forehead protector](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Forehead_protector "Forehead protector"), given to him by Iruka after graduating from the Academy. In Part II, Naruto wears a more form fitting outfit after wearing out his original one while training with Jiraiya,[^57] with a T-shirt or mesh armour underneath. While retaining the swirls and orange pants, the blue pieces of his outfit changes to black: his forehead protector (which is now longer), sandals (also longer), and orange jacket, the black colour showing more prominence than the blue did originally. During his battle with Pain, Naruto briefly wore a short-sleeved red coat with a black flame pattern around the hem and carried a large scroll on his back.

Naruto lost his right arm after the [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"), though it is later replaced by a fully manoeuvrable prosthetic arm made of [Hashirama Senju](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hashirama_Senju "Hashirama Senju") 's cells, wrapped completely in bandages. In *[The Last: Naruto the Movie](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Last:_Naruto_the_Movie "The Last: Naruto the Movie")*, Naruto grew again to be one of the tallest of his graduating class, cut his hair short, and wore a black elastic forehead protector. Over his pants and sandals, he wears a black uniform jacket with an orange zipper and buttons on the waist and sleeves, which can be folded up at times. He has a red armband with an Uzumaki crest on his left arm. After becoming Hokage, Naruto dons a garment similar to his father's: a white cape that has a red flame pattern around the hem, is held together by a red rope, and has the kanji for "Seventh Hokage" (七代目火影, *Nanadaime Hokage*) written vertically down the back. Underneath this, he wears an orange sweatshirt with black stripes (reverse in the manga version of *[Boruto: Naruto Next Generations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boruto:_Naruto_Next_Generations "Boruto: Naruto Next Generations")*), black pants (orange in the manga version), and black shin high sandals. He occasionally wears the traditional [Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage "Hokage") headpiece, but has stopped wearing a forehead protector.

## Abilities

![Naruto's combined senjutsu and tailed beast influence over his trademark techniques.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/0a/Naruto_Vs_Kaguya.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20160825112853)

Naruto's combined senjutsu and tailed beast influence over his trademark techniques.

Originally, Naruto was a rather inept ninja, failing to graduate from the [Academy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Academy "Academy") three times. Despite these shortcomings, he was noted to possess even greater potential than shinobi prodigies like [Sasuke Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Uchiha "Sasuke Uchiha") and [Kakashi Hatake](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakashi_Hatake "Kakashi Hatake").[^58] Naruto would grow to realize this potential through sheer determination and training, as his skills rapidly improved to defeat strong [genin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genin "Genin") like [Neji Hyūga](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Neji_Hy%C5%ABga "Neji Hyūga"), [Gaara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gaara "Gaara"), and [Kabuto Yakushi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kabuto_Yakushi "Kabuto Yakushi"), which even earned him acknowledgement from all the [Sannin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sannin "Sannin").[^59] From further training with renown shinobi like Jiraya, Kakashi, and Fukasaku, Naruto becomes strong enough to defeat [various](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakuzu "Kakuzu") [members](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nagato "Nagato") of [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki"), ultimately earning praise from Konoha as a Hokage-material hero.[^60] From [Killer B](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Killer_B "Killer B") ’s training and learning to control [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama") ’s power, Naruto later becomes capable of defeating various reincarnated [Kage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kage "Kage") [^61] and [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki") during the Fourth Shinobi World War,[^62] with many believing he was the central key to winning the battle.[^63] [^64] [^65] By adulthood, his prowess made him the Seventh Hokage, being regarded as the strongest shinobi in history,[^66] [^67] and having command of overwhelming [jutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jutsu "Jutsu").[^68] With the combined might of Sasuke Uchiha, noted as his only rival in power,[^40] they are said to be able to destroy the world.[^69] Together, they overpowered an [empowered](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_Edible_Creation "Chakra Edible Creation") - [Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Momoshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki"), and pushed [Jigen](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jigen "Jigen") 's body to its limit. Overall, he could compete against if not defeat world-threatening opponents, such as [Obito Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Obito_Uchiha "Obito Uchiha"), [Madara Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Madara_Uchiha "Madara Uchiha"), [Toneri Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Toneri_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Toneri Ōtsutsuki"), and [Kaguya Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kaguya_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Kaguya Ōtsutsuki"). When fighting at his [absolute peak](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Baryon_Mode "Baryon Mode"), he decisively overpowered [Isshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Isshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Isshiki Ōtsutsuki"). Even without the aid of Kurama's power, Naruto's prowess was still acknowledged as worthy of being the Seventh Hokage.

### Chakra and Physical Prowess

![Naruto sharing Kurama's chakra through physical contact.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/be/Naruto_gives_Hinata_power.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20140605131235)

Naruto sharing Kurama's chakra through physical contact.

Naruto possesses remarkably large [chakra](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra "Chakra") reserves as a result of being made [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama") ’s [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki") at birth, and constantly having portions of the fox’s immense chakra [naturally](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Four_Symbols_Seal "Four Symbols Seal") [mix](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Trigrams_Sealing_Style "Eight Trigrams Sealing Style") with his own.[^70] [^71] [^72] Even as a child, his reserves had at least four times the amount of a seasoned [Jōnin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/J%C5%8Dnin "Jōnin") like [Kakashi Hatake](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakashi_Hatake "Kakashi Hatake"),[^73] and described by [Karin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Karin "Karin") as "bright and warm".[^74] His initial poor chakra control was offset by his sheer reserves,[^75] which were large enough to endure three and a half days of constant battle against several powerful opponents. Only by the end of his battle against Sasuke did he show exhaustion, and even then could still fight to a standstill and eventual victory.[^76] Naruto's control improved greatly over time; able to [transfer his chakra](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_Transfer_Technique "Chakra Transfer Technique") to others,[^77] and perform one-handed hand seals.[^78] Being an Uzumaki, Naruto's powerful life-force grants him an extreme longevity, stamina, and vitality;[^79] this allowed him to survive the extraction of a tailed beast, though he required aid from [Sakura Haruno](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sakura_Haruno "Sakura Haruno") to stay alive.[^80] Naruto also learned how to counter [genjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genjutsu "Genjutsu") from [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya"), but his ability to do so is limited.[^81] In adulthood, Naruto's chakra is powerful enough to intimidate Kawaki into submission [^82] and destroy high-level barriers simply by releasing a burst of it.[^83] He could repel [Shikamaru Nara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shikamaru_Nara "Shikamaru Nara") 's [Shadow Imitation Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Imitation_Technique "Shadow Imitation Technique"), a feat that no one else could do.[^69] His chakra, when he combined it with his [son's](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boruto_Uzumaki "Boruto Uzumaki") [Vanishing Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Vanishing_Rasengan "Vanishing Rasengan"), turned it into a gigantic [Ultra-Big Ball Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ultra-Big_Ball_Rasengan "Ultra-Big Ball Rasengan") able to kill the [empowered](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_Edible_Creation "Chakra Edible Creation") [Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Momoshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Momoshiki Ōtsutsuki") who tried to use a [copy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Deep_Crimson_Spiral "Deep Crimson Spiral") of the technique to defeat his.[^84] His chakra reserves were also so vast that he was able to overload [Delta](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Delta "Delta") 's [Absorbing Eye](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Absorbing_Eye "Absorbing Eye") with a [continuous](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Super-Ultra-Big_Ball_Rasengan "Super-Ultra-Big Ball Rasengan") Rasengan-assault and still primed to defeat her.[^85]

![Naruto attacking Gaara with his Naruto Uzumaki Two Thousand Combo.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/c1/Naruto%27s_clones_vs_Gaara.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150221063055)

Naruto attacking Gaara with his Naruto Uzumaki Two Thousand Combo.

While naturally a short-range fighter, Naruto originally was inept at [taijutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Taijutsu "Taijutsu"), requiring unpredictable attacks to surprise combat experts like [Kiba Inuzuka](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kiba_Inuzuka "Kiba Inuzuka") and Neji Hyūga.[^86] [^87] He often utilised shadow clones in large if not massive numbers to simply overwhelm his foes.[^88] Although these tactics resumed later, Naruto becomes much more adept at taijutsu due to Jiraiya's training.[^89] After [Fukasaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fukasaku "Fukasaku") 's tutelage, he could fend off a pair of expert kenjutsu users.[^90] [^91] [^92] Two years after the war, Naruto's taijutsu prowess could best dozens of opponents without shadow clones or enhancements.[^23] As Hokage, his taijutsu could pressure Momoshiki after he had absorbed [Kinshiki Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kinshiki_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Kinshiki Ōtsutsuki").[^84] Naruto could even hold off Delta despite her [extreme body modifications](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shinobi-Ware "Shinobi-Ware"), ultimately controlling the pace of the battle to tire her out and gradually overwhelm her [^93] to win despite being in the defensive.[^85] His speed had also increased to the point where he could effortlessly catch up with [Kawaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kawaki "Kawaki") after he tried to escape from Naruto.[^82] Likewise, in the anime, he is proven very resilient, able to take a point-blank blast from Kawaki's modifications unfazed.[^94]

#### Body Modifications

![Naruto's prototype Absorbing Hand.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/ee/Shinobi_Ware_Absorption.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210111023937)

Naruto's prototype Absorbing Hand.

After losing his right arm during his final battle with Sasuke, [Tsunade](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsunade "Tsunade") cultured an artificial prosthesis for him using the cells of [Hashirama Senju](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hashirama_Senju "Hashirama Senju"). In addition to retaining the same mobility and dexterity as his original arm, it is likewise able to channel and manipulate chakra just as effectively. As his body and the prosthesis arms are only connected through his chakra itself he can easily remove and replace them.[^95] After Momoshiki's defeat, Naruto took inspiration from his [chakra absorption technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_absorption_technique "Chakra absorption technique") and had [Katasuke Tōno](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Katasuke_T%C5%8Dno "Katasuke Tōno") and the [Scientific Ninja Weapons Team](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Scientific_Ninja_Weapons_Team "Scientific Ninja Weapons Team") modifiy the arm further with mechanical features. With it, Naruto is able to [neutralise and even absorb](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Absorbing_Hand "Absorbing Hand") enemy techniques, though they have yet to replicate Momoshiki's ability to discharge them as a counterattack.[^95]

### Jinchūriki Transformations

Main articles: [Jinchūriki Forms](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms "Jinchūriki Forms"), [Nine-Tails Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails_Chakra_Mode "Nine-Tails Chakra Mode") and [Baryon Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Baryon_Mode "Baryon Mode")

![Naruto's Version 1 form.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/87/Version_1_form.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150223122400)

Naruto's Version 1 form.

Originally as the [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki") of [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama") 's Yang half, Naruto had access to massive reserves of exceptionally powerful chakra, which was at least a hundredfold greater than Kakashi's.[^73] These chakra levels were increased even further when the Yin half of Kurama was reunited with the Yang half inside of Naruto. Due to his Uzumaki blood and being Kurama's jinchūriki for essentially his entire life, Naruto's chakra had become naturally compatible with the fox's,[^96] allowing him to perform several chakra-taxing techniques unhindered. In addition, Kurama being inside him gave Naruto a passive form of healing, and when accessing it was enhanced to a level similar to Hashirama's regenerative abilities. Originally, he could only access Kurama's chakra in times of great anger and doing so enhanced Naruto's abilities. However, the fox's negative influence made Naruto more aggressive. While essentially remaining himself when accessing a [Version 1](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms#Version_1 "Jinchūriki Forms") state, manifesting four tails would trigger [Version 2](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms#Version_2 "Jinchūriki Forms") state, which made him lose control and would gradually turn into even more [feral](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Control "Tailed Beast Control") forms, requiring outside assistance to suppress Kurama's power and regain control, prompting him to avoid relying on the fox's power whenever possible. By the end of the Fourth Great Shinobi War, he could access his Version 1 form without any loss in his mentality.[^97]

![Naruto transforming from his Nine-Tails Chakra Mode into his Kurama Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/f/f7/Naruto_transforming.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20141026161814)

Naruto transforming from his Nine-Tails Chakra Mode into his Kurama Mode.

Realising he could not avoid using Kurama's power, Naruto sought to control it with help from [Killer B](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Killer_B "Killer B"), a jinchūriki who had fully mastered the power of [Gyūki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gy%C5%ABki "Gyūki"). Upon succeeding and taking most of Kurama's chakra, Naruto attained the [Nine-Tails Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails_Chakra_Mode "Nine-Tails Chakra Mode"), which greatly increased his physical parameters. Naruto can also detect [negative emotions](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Negative_Emotions_Sensing "Negative Emotions Sensing") in this mode, and use [Tailed Beast Chakra Arms](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Chakra_Arms "Tailed Beast Chakra Arms") from any part of his body as he pleases, as well as form [miniature Tailed Beast Balls](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Super_Mini-Tailed_Beast_Ball "Super Mini-Tailed Beast Ball"). As he didn't have Kurama's cooperation, Naruto needed to exercise extreme caution while using the form, as the chakra exchange could cost him his life. Eventually, when Kurama acknowledged Naruto's life-long strength of character and Naruto learned the fox's true name, their new-found friendship granted Naruto access to its [full power](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails_Chakra_Mode#Kurama_Mode "Nine-Tails Chakra Mode"), which further increased his physical parameters and with no drawbacks. This also increased his resilience to such degrees to the point where he could survive the [Ten-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ten-Tails "Ten-Tails") ' [Tenpenchii](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tenpenchii "Tenpenchii") and endure Toneri's [Golden Wheel Reincarnation Explosion](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Wheel_Reincarnation_Explosion "Golden Wheel Reincarnation Explosion") — which was able to cut the [Moon](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Moon "Moon") in half — with no visible signs of damage. This cooperation of Kurama also provides Naruto with an immediate resistance from [genjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genjutsu "Genjutsu"). Two years later, he could [store](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama_Arm_Attack "Kurama Arm Attack") Kurama's chakra within his arm to deliver powerful punches capable of creating massive craters on the Moon and depower Toneri from his [Tenseigan Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tenseigan_Chakra_Mode "Tenseigan Chakra Mode") to his normal state, as well as block the latter's Golden Wheel Reincarnation Explosion.

![Naruto's Tailed Beast Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/9/91/Naruto%27s_tailed_beast_mode.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150825070824)

Naruto's Tailed Beast Mode.

Failing to transform into a replica of Kurama at first, Naruto instead achieved a [Tailed Beast Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Mode "Tailed Beast Mode") in the unique form of a life-sized transparent avatar of chakra, through which all of his attacks become incredibly powerful.[^98] In it, he can use the [Tailed Beast Ball](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Ball "Tailed Beast Ball"), which by adulthood, is considered strong enough to destroy the entire planet,[^99] he could revitalise the life-forces of anyone he touches physically or with the avatar,[^100] [^101] heal nearly any wounds,[^40] or grant them temporarily chakra-cloaks.[^102] After he became the jinchūriki of Kurama's complete self, Naruto is able to manifest the demon fox outside himself for a period akin to summoning, allowing both to fight as separate entities. During the endgame of the Fourth Shinobi World War, Naruto received chakra from the other tailed beasts,[^103] which greatly increased his chakra reserves even further and allowed him to access their unique abilities himself, such as [Shukaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shukaku "Shukaku") 's [sand](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sand "Sand") manipulation, [Saiken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Saiken "Saiken") 's [soap bubble ninjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Soap_Bubble_Ninjutsu "Soap Bubble Ninjutsu") and ability to emit [corrosive liquids](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wisdom_Wolf_Decay "Wisdom Wolf Decay"), [Chōmei](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%8Dmei "Chōmei") 's [scale powder](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Scale_Powder "Scale Powder"), or [Gyūki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gy%C5%ABki "Gyūki") 's [ink creation](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ink_Creation "Ink Creation"), and infuse them into his own [techniques](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Art:_Super_Tailed_Beast_Rasenshuriken "Sage Art: Super Tailed Beast Rasenshuriken"). With the infusion of all nine of the tailed beasts' respective chakra inside of him, Naruto has become a medium to connect all the tailed beasts to communicate with each other [^104] and made him a human pillar for the [power of the Ten-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Senjutsu "Six Paths Senjutsu").[^105]

![Naruto's Baryon Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/1/17/Naruto%27s_Baryon_Mode.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210926184438)

Naruto's Baryon Mode.

In the most dire of situations, Naruto is able to access a deep level of Kurama's power, [Baryon Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Baryon_Mode "Baryon Mode"). In this state, his facial features become even more akin to Kurama with nine blazing chakra tails which he can use in a similar manner to Tailed Beast Chakra Arms to catch and bind opponents or objects and [deflect attacks](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Baryon_Tail_Breaker "Baryon Tail Breaker"). He also displayed the ability to use these chakra tails to form a protective shield around something or someone.[^106] In a process similar to [nuclear fusion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nuclear_fusion "wikipedia:Nuclear fusion"), Naruto's and [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama") 's chakra are consumed as raw materials to produce a new type of energy. When in use, it dramatically boosts Naruto's reflexes, raw might, and chakra to such extreme levels, able to surpass Isshiki's capabilities. Because all chakra is connected, the same power that is produced from bits of their lives also work to reduce the lifespan of opponents on contact. Due to its extreme energy consumption, Naruto must remain focused and not waste energy on extraneous thoughts and actions, to maximise the amount of time he can maintain Baryon Mode.[^107] The drawback of this form is the high chance of death for Kurama from the extreme strain of this form. Upon Kurama's death from this form, while Naruto was spared the same fate, he lost access to all of Kurama's chakra and abilities.[^108]

### Ninjutsu

![Naruto using the Summoning Technique.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/6/6a/Summoning_Technique.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150120115239)

Naruto using the Summoning Technique.

In the Academy, the [Sexy Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy_Technique "Sexy Technique") was the only technique that Naruto could perform successfully, which had no practical use other than to shock people. Later, he learned more advanced and useful ninjutsu from [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya"), showing remarkable growth in his ninjutsu skill, which can be observed even from his first brief exchange after reuniting with Kakashi, even getting beyond the jōnin via skillful use of ninjutsu.[^3] This included [summoning](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Summoning_Technique "Summoning Technique") [toads](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Toads "Toads") from Mount Myōboku to assist him, and use them to [crush](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Summoning:_Food_Cart_Destroyer_Technique "Summoning: Food Cart Destroyer Technique") enemies from above. He became skilled in [shurikenjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shurikenjutsu "Shurikenjutsu"), able to throw weapons quickly and with precision.[^109] Naruto's repertoire expanded to [fūinjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/F%C5%ABinjutsu "Fūinjutsu"), where he could [store](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Enclosing_Technique "Enclosing Technique") his weapons within scrolls. His fūinjutsu was further increased upon receiving [Gerotora](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gerotora "Gerotora") near the start of the Fourth Shinobi World War.[^110] In the anime, Jiraiya taught Naruto [cooperation ninjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Cooperation_ninjutsu "Cooperation ninjutsu"), combining his techniques with others to create an even more powerful one.[^111] The collaboration techniques he performed with other individuals typically utilised his own elemental affinity, such as the [Typhoon Water Vortex Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Typhoon_Water_Vortex_Technique "Typhoon Water Vortex Technique") with [Yamato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yamato "Yamato"), the [Wind Release: Toad Gun](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Toad_Gun "Wind Release: Toad Gun") with [Gamatatsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamatatsu "Gamatatsu"), and the [Scorch Release: Halo Hurricane Jet Black Arrow Style Zero](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Scorch_Release:_Halo_Hurricane_Jet_Black_Arrow_Style_Zero "Scorch Release: Halo Hurricane Jet Black Arrow Style Zero") with Sasuke. He also learned from [Asuma Sarutobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Asuma_Sarutobi "Asuma Sarutobi") to [flow](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra_Flow "Chakra Flow") chakra into his weapons to increase their offensive might.[^112] In the anime, by adulthood, Naruto became able to use the [Shuriken Shadow Clone Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shuriken_Shadow_Clone_Technique "Shuriken Shadow Clone Technique"),[^113] and also showed some proficiency with bōjutsu.[^114]

#### Shadow Clone Technique

![Naruto using the Multiple Shadow Clone Technique.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/e6/Multiple_Shadow_Clone_Technique.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150221104302)

Naruto using the Multiple Shadow Clone Technique.

*See also: [Images of Naruto using Shadow Clone Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Special:RunQuery/Image_query?Image_query%5Bcharacters%5D=Naruto&Image_query%5Bjutsu%5D=Shadow%20Clone%20Technique&wpRunQuery=true "Images of Naruto using Shadow Clone Technique")* Naruto's first trademark technique was the [Shadow Clone Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Clone_Technique "Shadow Clone Technique"). While originally failing constantly with a basic illusionary clone, after briefly studying the [Scroll of Seals](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Scroll_of_Seals "Scroll of Seals"), he learned to create shadow clones on a [mass scale](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Multiple_Shadow_Clone_Technique "Multiple Shadow Clone Technique"). From then on, Naruto's skill with shadow clones blossomed to great heights. Having unusually high chakra reserves, Naruto could use this technique to create hundreds of shadow clones and retain large amounts of chakra in each one with relative ease.[^115] He could use them to outnumber or deceive his enemies, manoeuvre himself in mid-air, transform into weapons, scout areas, or test an opponent's abilities.[^116] During his elemental-affinity training, learning that the original will gain all the knowledge and conditioning of a shadow clone right after it dispersed. From this, Naruto can use his mass scale of clones to drastically speed up his training to gain years worth of experience in just days. By the time he became Hokage, Naruto's usage of this technique on mass-scale and prolonged-periods allowed him to constantly perform multiple simultaneous tasks throughout the village daily, although he does have a tendency to overexert himself, leaving him exhausted afterwards.

#### Rasengan

![Naruto using the Rasengan.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d6/Naruto%27s_Rasengan.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20191003092311)

Naruto using the Rasengan.

Naruto's second trademark technique is the [Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rasengan "Rasengan"). Originally, due to his poor chakra control, Naruto had to use a shadow clone to form the spherical shape while he provided the chakra. Over time, Naruto developed [larger](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Ball_Rasengan "Big Ball Rasengan") [versions](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ultra-Big_Ball_Rasengan "Ultra-Big Ball Rasengan") of the Rasengan and learned how to perform it faster.[^117] During the Fourth Shinobi World War, he learned to use the Rasengan and its variants unaided with a single hand, or even form one in [both hands simultaneously](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Spiralling_Serial_Spheres "Spiralling Serial Spheres").[^118] He also increased the size of his standard Rasengan.[^119] Naruto had also shown the ability to release the Rasengan as an energy wave,[^23] or as a projectile in the anime.[^120] Using senjutsu or Kurama's chakra, Naruto can create [more](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Technique:_Spiralling_Serial_Spheres "Sage Technique: Spiralling Serial Spheres") [powerful](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Spiralling_Strife_Spheres "Spiralling Strife Spheres") and [elaborate](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Spiralling_Absorption_Sphere "Spiralling Absorption Sphere") [variations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Planetary_Rasengan "Planetary Rasengan") of the Rasengan. In the anime, usage of his tailed beast chakra also allows him to instantly perform massive Rasengan without the aid of his hands and launch in quick successions, similar to a [Tailed Beast Ball](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Ball "Tailed Beast Ball").[^121]

#### Nature Transformation

![Naruto simultaneously using Wind and Lava Release.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d8/Naruto_Creating_Lava_Rasenshuriken.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150813115318)

Naruto simultaneously using Wind and Lava Release.

Naruto's natural affinity is [Wind Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release "Wind Release"). Using shadow clones, one to form the Rasengan while another infuses the Wind chakra, Naruto could complete the difficult task of producing a Rasengan that is the pinnacle of shape and nature transformation, something no one before him could accomplish, including Kakashi and his father. Once adjusting to using the [Wind Release: Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Rasengan "Wind Release: Rasengan") he could expand upon it to produce the [Wind Release: Rasenshuriken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Rasenshuriken "Wind Release: Rasenshuriken"). He also created [different](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mini-Rasenshuriken "Mini-Rasenshuriken") - [sized](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Ultra-Big_Ball_Rasenshuriken "Wind Release: Ultra-Big Ball Rasenshuriken") [variations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Ball_Rasenshuriken "Big Ball Rasenshuriken"), and could even use [two Rasenshuriken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Repeated_Rasenshuriken "Wind Release: Repeated Rasenshuriken") simultaneously. Two years after its creation, he became skilled enough to form a Rasenshuriken without any shadow clones and throw it in his base form,[^23] a feat which he previously could only do while using his enhanced modes.

After acquiring [Six Paths](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Senjutsu "Six Paths Senjutsu") [abilities](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Sage_Mode "Six Paths Sage Mode"),[^122] Naruto gains access to all five basic [nature transformations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nature_transformation "Nature transformation"), as well as [Yang Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yang_Release "Yang Release").[^123] He could temporarily utilize [Yin–Yang Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yin%E2%80%93Yang_Release "Yin–Yang Release") to revitalize life-forces, heal whomever he touches, and restore missing organs.[^124] After gaining chakra from the respective tailed beasts, Naruto can also use [Shukaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shukaku "Shukaku") 's [Magnet Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Magnet_Release "Magnet Release"), [Son Gokū](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Son_Gok%C5%AB "Son Gokū") 's [Lava Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Lava_Release "Lava Release"), and [Kokuō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Koku%C5%8D "Kokuō") 's [Boil Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boil_Release "Boil Release"), able to infuse any of the other elements into is normal techniques as seamlessly as with Wind chakra. Having an arm made from Hashirama's cells, Naruto has a possibility to use [Wood Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wood_Release "Wood Release").[^125]

### Senjutsu

![Naruto's Sage Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/dc/Naruto%27s_Sage_Mode.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150124180545)

Naruto's Sage Mode.

Naruto later trained in [senjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Senjutsu "Senjutsu") at Mount Myōboku, which was only possible due to his high chakra reserves. Unlike [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya"), he was able to perfectly balance [natural energy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Natural_energy "Natural energy") with his chakra, and enter a complete [Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Mode "Sage Mode"), symbolised by the orange pigmentation around his yellow eyes, his toad-like pupils, and no other alterations to his appearance. Entering this state further enhances Naruto's physical prowess and techniques.[^126] [^127] [^128] It also grants him access to the [Frog Kata](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Frog_Kata "Frog Kata") taijutsu style,[^129] and the ability to [sense](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sensing_Technique "Sensing Technique") chakra to the degree of identifying different signatures from vast distances,[^130] and determining a target’s strength level.[^131] Since [Fukasaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fukasaku "Fukasaku") was unable to [fuse](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Art:_Amphibian_Technique "Sage Art: Amphibian Technique") with Naruto and gather natural energy for him due to Kurama's interference, Naruto had to employ shadow clones instead to maintain the technique, taking advantage of the clones' own buildup of natural energy being transferred to the original and let him use [Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Mode "Sage Mode") a number of times. However, creating more than five shadow clones overall would disrupt the focus of the clones gathering natural energy. The need for this method diminishes over time as Naruto became capable of entering Sage Mode much faster.[^132] However, after finding unity with [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama"), Naruto overcame this problem and learned to combine Sage Mode with Kurama's chakra to further enhance his abilities, such that he could resist [Nagato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nagato "Nagato") 's control and shatter [Truth-Seeking Balls](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Truth-Seeking_Ball "Truth-Seeking Ball"). In later years, having drawn experience from his various events, Naruto became able to maintain Sage Mode for much longer durations unaided.

#### Six Paths Senjutsu

Main article: [Six Paths Senjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Senjutsu "Six Paths Senjutsu") Naruto also gained access to the **Six Paths Senjutsu** (六道仙術, *Rikudō Senjutsu*), which allows him to fly and also manifest up to nine [Truth-Seeking Balls](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Truth-Seeking_Ball "Truth-Seeking Ball"), composed of all five basic [natures](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nature "Nature"), use Yin–Yang Release, and imbue them with the [Six Paths Chakra](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Chakra "Six Paths Chakra"). He can mould the balls into a myriad of shapes, fire them as projectiles and still control them from distance, or convert them into Tailed Beast Balls and use them to create [Tailed Beast Ball Rasenshurikens](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Ball_Rasenshuriken "Tailed Beast Ball Rasenshuriken") capable of destroying the planetoids generated from [Chibaku Tensei](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chibaku_Tensei "Chibaku Tensei"). While his shadow clones are unable to produce Truth-Seeking Balls of their own, Naruto is able to transfer control of his own to any of his shadow clones.

#### Six Paths Sage Mode

Main article: [Six Paths Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Sage_Mode "Six Paths Sage Mode")

![Naruto's Six Paths Sage Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/2/22/Naruto%27s_SPSM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150730222303)

Naruto's Six Paths Sage Mode.

With the power given to him by [Hagoromo Ōtsutsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hagoromo_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Hagoromo Ōtsutsuki"), Naruto gained access to the **[Six Paths Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_Sage_Mode "Six Paths Sage Mode")** (六道仙人モード, *Rikudō Sennin Mōdo*).[^5] In this mode, his pupils take on a cross-like shape — without manifesting the orange pigmentation around his eyes present in Sage Mode. Going further, Naruto donned a new Nine-Tails Chakra Mode cloak, which he could access instantly and maintain much longer than his standard Sage Mode.[^133] Naruto's physical attributes and techniques are further augmented,[^134] to the point where he could dodge [attacks](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Art:_Storm_Release_Light_Fang "Sage Art: Storm Release Light Fang") that moved at the speed of light and deflect Truth-Seeking Balls. It also empowers his sensory abilities to the highest possible level,[^135] which allowed him to sense the [invisible shadows](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Limbo:_Border_Jail "Limbo: Border Jail") of Limbo.

![Naruto's Asura Kurama Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/a5/Asura_Kurama_Mode.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20160929122359)

Naruto's Asura Kurama Mode.

Naruto also retained use of his Tailed Beast Mode avatar of Kurama, which rivalled Sasuke's Rinnegan-empowered [Complete Body — Susanoo](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Susanoo "Susanoo") in size and power.[^136] Using two shadow clones, he could create and combine three Kurama avatars into a single construct with three faces and six arms, which is largely reminiscent of Asura's [Six Paths: Kunitsukami](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths:_Kunitsukami "Six Paths: Kunitsukami").[^137] This state allowed Naruto to infuse [two massive Rasenshuriken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths:_Ultra-Big_Ball_Rasenshuriken "Six Paths: Ultra-Big Ball Rasenshuriken") with natural energy whose destructive capabilities rivalled that of Sasuke's most powerful jutsu, [Indra's Arrow](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Indra%27s_Arrow "Indra's Arrow");[^138] the [resulting explosion](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths:_Shinra_Tenky%C5%8D "Six Paths: Shinra Tenkyō") from the two techniques colliding created an enormous cyclone that was visible from outer space.[^139]

### Intelligence

![Naruto reveals himself after disguising as a Wind Release: Rasenshuriken.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d2/Naruto%27s_intelligence.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150220083928)

Naruto reveals himself after disguising as a Wind Release: Rasenshuriken.

While headstrong and often acting without thinking, Naruto's years as a prankster had given him a cunning imagination that was useful in split-second decision making, which has repeatedly proved invaluable in battle. He is a remarkable [tactile learner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kinesthetic_learning "wikipedia:Kinesthetic learning"), able to learn better through executing a task rather than understanding the theory of it.[^140] [^27] Jiraiya also considered him to have a good gambling instinct.[^141] Once seen in action, even the likes of the Second Hokage revise their thoughts about Naruto's intellect and instinct in battle.[^142] After meeting Hagoromo Ōtsutsuki, Naruto gained the innate ability to grasp the nature of chakra and comprehend all universal things,[^143] allowing him to quickly master the new powers he received. By adulthood he had become a capable teacher, instructing students at Konoha's [Ninja Academy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ninja_Academy "Ninja Academy").[^144] [^145] During his fight with [Delta](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Delta "Delta"), Naruto showed his ability to think ahead by hiding his power and faking defeat in order to make Delta drop her guard and reveal her plans, although Delta was perceptive enough to see through his attempts. Naruto ultimately was able to find out the weakness of Delta's [Shinobi-Ware](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shinobi-Ware "Shinobi-Ware") absorbing eye and overtax it after seeing it in action repeatedly.[^85]

Naruto's most famous mental skill is to deceive his opponents, as he made effective use of even the most [simple techniques](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Transformation_Technique "Transformation Technique") to trick the [deadliest of opponents](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_of_Pain "Six Paths of Pain").[^146] He could formulate multi-step plans or backup plans in the thick of battle,[^147] and act quickly even when given new information.[^148] He can also seemingly make himself predictable by creating a pattern, only to throw off his opponent by breaking it. He has even thrown in what others would call [completely useless techniques](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy_Reverse_Harem_Technique "Sexy Reverse Harem Technique") at [powerful opponents](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kaguya_%C5%8Ctsutsuki "Kaguya Ōtsutsuki") to create an opening for a counterattack.[^149] Naruto is rather observant, able to notice details others may overlook and subsequently take advantage of them to overcome his opponents.[^150] As an adult, Naruto proved to be a capable teacher in ninjutsu, quickly teaching the basics to [Kawaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kawaki "Kawaki").

## Part I

*See also: [Plot of Naruto](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Plot_of_Naruto "Plot of Naruto")*

### Prologue — Land of Waves

Main article: [Prologue — Land of Waves](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Prologue_%E2%80%94_Land_of_Waves "Prologue — Land of Waves")

![Naruto is protected by Iruka.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d8/Iruka_protects_naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150504035704)

Naruto is protected by Iruka.

Failing once again to graduate from the [Academy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Academy "Academy"), a disappointed Naruto is advised by one of his instructors, [Mizuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mizuki "Mizuki"), to steal the [Scroll of Seals](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Scroll_of_Seals "Scroll of Seals") and learn a technique from it in order to graduate. As Naruto struggled to learn the [Shadow Clone Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Clone_Technique "Shadow Clone Technique"), [Iruka Umino](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Iruka_Umino "Iruka Umino") tracked him down and realised that Naruto was tricked by Mizuki into stealing the scroll. Mizuki attacked them and told Naruto about the Nine-Tails sealed within him, claiming that Iruka hated him because of it. When Iruka risked his life to protect Naruto, however, he saw through Mizuki's lies and used the [Multiple Shadow Clone Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Multiple_Shadow_Clone_Technique "Multiple Shadow Clone Technique") to defeat him, prompting Iruka to happily grant Naruto his graduation from the Academy. Naruto would later befriend [Konohamaru Sarutobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konohamaru_Sarutobi "Konohamaru Sarutobi"), grandson of the [Third Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Third_Hokage "Third Hokage"), and teach him several perverted or useful techniques.

![Team 7's group photo.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/5/50/Team_Kakashi.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20161219035928)

Team 7's group photo.

Naruto was eventually assigned to [Team 7](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Kakashi "Team Kakashi"), partnered with [Sasuke Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Uchiha "Sasuke Uchiha") and [Sakura Haruno](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sakura_Haruno "Sakura Haruno") under the leadership of [Kakashi Hatake](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakashi_Hatake "Kakashi Hatake"). During their first meeting, Naruto shared his love of ramen, his hobbies, and his dream to become Hokage. To test their qualifications, Kakashi gave the team a [bell test](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Bell_test "Bell test"), stating that whichever of the three takes one of the two bells on his person will officially become [genin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genin "Genin"). Instead of hiding like Sakura and Sasuke, Naruto tried to take the bells from Kakashi by force, only to be easily defeated, hung upside-down from a tree and tied to a wooden post in an attempt to steal lunch. After Sakura and Sasuke fail as well, Kakashi explains that the goal of the test was to use teamwork, to do together what none of them could do by themselves. He is persuaded to allow them to try again after lunch, but instructs Sasuke and Sakura not to feed Naruto. They feed him anyway, needing him in top-form if they're to work together. Kakashi sees this and, because they care more about the team than listening to his instructions, allows them all to pass.

![Naruto and Sasuke team up against Zabuza.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/ba/Zabuza_Vs_Genin.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20151204035743)

Naruto and Sasuke team up against Zabuza.

After a series of uneventful [D-rank](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/D-rank "D-rank") missions, Naruto is able to secure a [C-rank](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/C-rank "C-rank") mission for Team 7: escorting [Tazuna](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tazuna "Tazuna") to the [Land of Waves](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Waves "Land of Waves"). Soon after leaving Konoha, they are attacked by the [Demon Brothers](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Demon_Brothers "Demon Brothers"). Naruto is paralysed with fear, forcing Sasuke to step in to disarm them and protect Tazuna until Kakashi can capture them. Tazuna confesses that assassins have been hired to kill him but that he couldn't afford the bodyguard detail he needs. Although the mission is now [A-rank](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/A-rank "A-rank") in nature - far beyond the skill of genin - Team 7 decides to continue with it. Angered by Sasuke's taunting of him, Naruto cuts his hand to bleed out the poison he received earlier, vowing to never waver again. When they arrive in the [Land of Waves](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Waves "Land of Waves") and are confronted by [Zabuza Momochi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Zabuza_Momochi "Zabuza Momochi"), Naruto is overwhelmed by the battle between Kakashi and Zabuza before he is knocked aside by Zabuza's [water clone](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Water_clone "Water clone"). Remembering his vow, Naruto regained his confidence and teamed up with Sasuke to free Kakashi from Zabuza's [Water Prison](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Water_Prison "Water Prison"). In the end, Zabuza is seemingly killed by [Haku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Haku "Haku"), allowing Team 7 to escort Tazuna back to his house.

Kakashi finds Zabuza's death suspicious and decides to train the team in case he returns. He has them perform the [Tree Climbing Practice](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tree_Climbing_Practice "Tree Climbing Practice") to improve their chakra control, which will help them against Zabuza. Naruto becomes frustrated after several failures and asks for advice from Sakura, who herself mastered it from the start. Now rapidly improving, Naruto competes with Sasuke to finish the training, each determined to outdo the other. One day, Naruto encounters Haku (albeit unaware of his true identity) and they each discussed their dreams and desire to protect those precious to them. After Haku leaves, Naruto finishes the training with Sasuke but is left exhausted, so Team 7 leaves him behind the next morning as they resume their escort duties. Naruto awakens and arrives to assist Team 7 in fighting Zabuza and Haku, but, unaware of how Haku's [Demonic Mirroring Ice Crystals](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Demonic_Mirroring_Ice_Crystals "Demonic Mirroring Ice Crystals") work, he joins Sasuke within the prison.

![Naruto's first use of the Nine-Tails' chakra.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/9/99/Naruto_Holds_Sasuke.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221003120339)

Naruto's first use of the Nine-Tails' chakra.

Naruto cannot break free with his shadow clones and Sasuke cannot melt the ice with his fire. As Haku moves in to kill Naruto, Sasuke uses his body as a shield to protect Naruto and seemingly dies. Enraged by Sasuke's apparent death, Naruto unwittingly accesses the [Nine-Tails' chakra](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms#Initial_Jinch%C5%ABriki_Form "Jinchūriki Forms") for the first time, allowing him to destroy the ice mirrors and defeat Haku, breaking his mask. Realising his opponent was the boy he met before, Naruto calms himself. He is asked by Haku to kill him, since he feels of no further use to Zabuza. Before Naruto can do so, Haku immediately stops him and goes off to save Zabuza from being killed by Kakashi, sacrificing his own life. When Zabuza refuses to appreciate this, Naruto angrily scolds him, stating that Haku gave up his life to save someone precious to him. Touched by his words, Zabuza, using Naruto's kunai, killed Gatō and many of his henchmen before he himself dies. Sasuke soon awakens, and when their injuries heal, Team 7 leaves for home via Tazuna's newly-constructed [Great Naruto Bridge](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Naruto_Bridge "Great Naruto Bridge").

### Chūnin Exams

Main article: [Chūnin Exams](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%ABnin_Exams_\(Arc\) "Chūnin Exams (Arc)")

![Naruto refuses to give up from the Chūnin Exams.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/3/34/Naruto_refuses_to_give_up.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240918025149)

Naruto refuses to give up from the Chūnin Exams.

Team 7 resumes its series of unremarkable missions. For their performance in the Land of Waves, however, Kakashi decides to enter them in the [Chūnin Exams](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%ABnin_Exams "Chūnin Exams") taking place in Konoha, which greatly excites Naruto. Because they've only recently graduated from the Academy, the three feel they must give strong showings to prove themselves. When they enter the exam hall, the team is met by [Rock Lee](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rock_Lee "Rock Lee"), who challenges Sasuke to a fight; Naruto tries to attack Lee in jealousy, but is easily swept aside. The fight is interrupted by [Might Guy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Guy "Might Guy"), after which Naruto notes that the bandages around Lee's arms signify his arduous taijutsu training while also mocking Sasuke for his unability to best Lee.

During the exam's first stage, the participating genin are given a written test, the goal of which is to cheat without getting caught. Unaware of this, Naruto struggles to answer the questions, so [Hinata Hyūga](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hinata_Hy%C5%ABga "Hinata Hyūga"), who is seated next to him, offers to let him copy off her paper. Despite the temptation, Naruto declined Hinata's offer, claiming that he isn't the type to cheat and that they might be disqualified if they are caught. Near the end, exam proctor [Ibiki Morino](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ibiki_Morino "Ibiki Morino"), explains to the genin that answering the tenth question incorrectly will bar the genin from ever retaking the exam. Before the tenth question can be given, the genin are presented with the opportunity to forfeit, which also automatically disqualifies the other members of their team; Naruto refuses to do so, declaring his refusal to give up and his goal to still become Hokage should he fail here. His determination to face the tenth question despite the potential consequences inspires the rest of the genin to do the same. This resolve is revealed to be the true test, and so they pass the first stage. It is later noted that Naruto was the only passing genin to hand in a blank sheet.

![Naruto saves Sasuke from Orochimaru's snake.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/f/f4/Naruto_saves_Sasuke_from_Orochimaru.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240907023924)

Naruto saves Sasuke from Orochimaru's snake.

For the second phase, teams enter the [Forest of Death](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Forest_of_Death "Forest of Death") with the objective of obtaining a set of two scrolls, one of which they are given at the start. Naruto becomes separated from his team and is attacked and swallowed by a giant snake. He manages to kill it and regroups with his frightened teammates against [Orochimaru](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Orochimaru "Orochimaru"). Angered by Sasuke's sudden cowardice and resignation, Naruto engages Orochimaru using the Nine-Tails' chakra, defeats his giant snake, and taunts Sasuke before Orochimaru [suppresses](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Five_Element_Seal "Five Element Seal") the Nine-Tails' chakra, rendering Naruto unconscious. After he awakens, Team 7 continues its search for the second scroll, which they eventually gain by defeating [Team Oboro](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Oboro "Team Oboro"), allowing them to pass the second stage.

![Naruto defeats Kiba.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/b5/Uzumakirendan.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240908054437)

Naruto defeats Kiba.

In the preliminary matches of the exam, Naruto was pitted against [Kiba Inuzuka](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kiba_Inuzuka "Kiba Inuzuka") and his dog, [Akamaru](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akamaru "Akamaru"). Believing the match to be an easy victory, Kiba and Akamaru overwhelmed Naruto with many high-speed attacks, so Naruto tricks Kiba into knocking Akamaru out of the fight by using the [Transformation Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Transformation_Technique "Transformation Technique"), shocking all spectators. Naruto then disorients Kiba by farting in his face and defeats him with the [Naruto Uzumaki Combo](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Uzumaki_Combo "Naruto Uzumaki Combo") (which he invented from watching Sasuke's [Lion Combo](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Lion_Combo "Lion Combo")). When Neji and Hinata's match begins, Naruto is enraged by Neji's ruthless tirade against Hinata and cheers Hinata on to defeat Neji. Although Hinata is defeated, Naruto wipes up her blood and vows to defeat Neji in the finals.

![Naruto meets the Nine-Tails.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/9/99/Naruto_meets_Kurama.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222031656)

Naruto meets the Nine-Tails.

During the month of training, Naruto first meets [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya") (who knocked out [Ebisu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ebisu "Ebisu"), Naruto's original teacher) and trains under the [Sannin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sannin "Sannin") to improve his chakra control. Recognising Naruto as the Nine-Tails' jinchūriki, Jiraiya [removes](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Five_Elements_Unseal "Five Elements Unseal") the seal Orochimaru had placed on Naruto to ease his control and begins teaching him how to use the Nine-Tails' power by [summoning](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Summoning_Technique "Summoning Technique") [toads](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Toads "Toads"). To accelerate Naruto's slow progress, Jiraiya pushes him over a cliff, causing Naruto to enter his subconscious and meet the Nine-Tails. Working past his fear, he bravely demands chakra from it as "rent" for living in his body. The Nine-Tails complies and Naruto summons [Gamabunta](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamabunta "Gamabunta"), though he exhausts himself and ends up in the [hospital](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_Hospital "Konoha Hospital"), where he is visited by [Shikamaru Nara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shikamaru_Nara "Shikamaru Nara"). After talking, he and Naruto stop [Gaara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gaara "Gaara") from killing [Rock Lee](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rock_Lee "Rock Lee") in the next room and listen to Gaara's story of his childhood, which Naruto finds very similar to his own. Gaara prepares to kill them, but [Might Guy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Guy "Might Guy") intervenes and forces Gaara to retreat. On the day of the finals, Naruto meets Hinata at the [Third Training Ground](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Third_Training_Ground "Third Training Ground") and expresses his doubts about his upcoming match with Neji. Hinata reassures Naruto that he never gave up because he always had the strength to overcome his own failures, admiring him for it. Reinvigorated, Naruto thanks Hinata and tells her even though he first thought she was weird, he now likes her.

![Naruto vs. Neji.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/ef/Naruto_vs_Neji.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150109174006)

Naruto vs. Neji.

Facing Neji in the first match of the finals, Naruto began by creating shadow clones to overwhelm Neji's with sheer numbers. Deeming Naruto a failure who could never defeat a genius like himself, Neji easily defeats Naruto's clones and seals his chakra with [Eight Trigrams Sixty-Four Palms](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Trigrams_Sixty-Four_Palms "Eight Trigrams Sixty-Four Palms"). Determined to prove Neji's ideals about fate wrong, Naruto tapped into the Nine-Tails' chakra and, revitalised, clashed with Neji. When the smoke cleared, Neji emerges and Naruto lies defeated. This, however, was only a shadow clone, and the real Naruto burst from the ground beneath Neji, defeating him with an uppercut. Before he is declared the winner, Naruto tells Neji that creating clones was once his shortcoming and that Neji needs to stop believing in inescapable fate. When Sasuke finally arrives for his match with Gaara, Naruto is envious of Sasuke's improvements, but is later [put to sleep](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Temple_of_Nirvana_Technique "Temple of Nirvana Technique") along with most of the audience, commencing the [Konoha Crush](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_Crush "Konoha Crush").

### Konoha Crush

Main article: [Konoha Crush](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_Crush_\(Arc\) "Konoha Crush (Arc)")

![Naruto saves Sasuke from Gaara.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/9/93/Naruto_saves_Sasuke_from_Gaara.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20251018194519)

Naruto saves Sasuke from Gaara.

Sakura wakes up Naruto so they can pursue Sasuke, who was pursuing Gaara himself. They arrive in time for Naruto to kick Gaara away before he can kill Sasuke. Partially transformed into [Shukaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shukaku "Shukaku"), Gaara knocks Sakura unconscious and binds her to a tree, forcing Naruto to battle him, with little success. Naruto relates to Gaara's painful life as a jinchūriki, but he is not willing to let anything to happen to Sakura and Sasuke.
