Determined to protect his friends, Naruto creates an army of shadow clones to [relentlessly batter](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Uzumaki_Two_Thousand_Combo "Naruto Uzumaki Two Thousand Combo") Gaara, forcing him to [fully transform](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Mode "Tailed Beast Mode") into Shukaku, to which Naruto responds by summoning Gamabunta.

![Naruto defeats Gaara.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/a1/Naruto_defeats_gaara.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240815025659)

Naruto defeats Gaara.

Gaara [puts himself to sleep](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Feigning_Sleep_Technique "Feigning Sleep Technique") to give control of his body to Shukaku, forcing Naruto and Gamabunta to figure out a way to end the jutsu. They do so by [transforming](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Combination_Transformation "Combination Transformation") into a giant fox (the Nine-Tails in the anime) to restrain Shukaku, allowing Naruto to awaken Gaara with a solid punch to the face. Shukaku's influence disappears, but Gaara regains control and traps Naruto with his sand. Naruto escapes by tapping into the Nine-Tails' chakra, and headbutts Gaara when he is close enough, destroying Shukaku's form. Exhausted from the fight, the two leap at each other for one final exchange, and Naruto punches Gaara, claiming victory.

Naruto and Gaara fell to the ground, unable to move. Slowly crawling towards Gaara, Naruto explained that he also suffered a painful and lonely life, but was later saved by having friends. To that end, he will do anything to protect them, even if it means killing Gaara. Understanding Naruto's true strength, Gaara retreated with [Kankurō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kankur%C5%8D "Kankurō") and [Temari](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Temari "Temari") with a new outlook on life, while Naruto passes out from exhaustion and is returned to Konoha with Sasuke and a rescued Sakura. A few days later, Team 7 attended the [Third Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Third_Hokage "Third Hokage") 's funeral.

### Search for Tsunade

Main article: [Search for Tsunade](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Search_for_Tsunade "Search for Tsunade") Jiraiya is tasked with finding [Tsunade](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsunade "Tsunade"), a candidate for Fifth Hokage, and convinces Naruto to accompany him by promising to teach him a technique stronger than Sasuke's [Chidori](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chidori "Chidori"). Stopping at an inn in [Shukuba Town](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shukuba_Town "Shukuba Town"), Naruto was approached by [Kisame Hoshigaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kisame_Hoshigaki "Kisame Hoshigaki") and [Itachi Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Itachi_Uchiha "Itachi Uchiha") of [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki"), who intend to capture the Nine-Tails. When Sasuke shows up and furiously attacks Itachi, Naruto gathers the Nine-Tails' chakra to help Sasuke, but it is absorbed by Kisame's [Samehada](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Samehada "Samehada"). Jiraiya soon arrives and drives off Itachi and Kisame, but Sasuke is left mentally and physically damaged by Itachi. [Might Guy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Guy "Might Guy") appears and takes Sasuke back to Konoha, but not before giving Naruto a spare green jumpsuit.

![Naruto and Tsunade face-off.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/8d/Tsunade_vs_naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20240927015621)

Naruto and Tsunade face-off.

To prepare for the next encounter with Akatsuki, Naruto begins learning the [Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rasengan "Rasengan"), completing two of its three learning steps. Taking a break from training, Naruto and Jiraiya eventually found Tsunade and her assistant [Shizune](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shizune "Shizune") at a restaurant. When Tsunade refuses the offer to become Hokage and insults all who held the title, an angry Naruto challenged her to a fight and attacked her with an incomplete Rasengan, only to be easily defeated. Impressed by his progress, however, Tsunade makes a bet with Naruto: if he can master the Rasengan in a week, he gets the [First Hokage's Necklace](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/First_Hokage%27s_Necklace "First Hokage's Necklace"); if not, she gets his [wallet](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gama-chan "Gama-chan"). Naruto agreed and spent the week trying to perfect the Rasengan, but has no success and collapses from exhaustion. Once he recovers, Naruto heads out with Jiraiya and Shizune to stop Tsunade from meeting with Orochimaru.

![Naruto defeats Kabuto.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/b0/Naruto_vs._Kabuto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150223120149)

Naruto defeats Kabuto.

They arrive to see Tsunade, who never intended to aid Orochimaru, in the midst of battle with [Kabuto Yakushi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kabuto_Yakushi "Kabuto Yakushi"). When Tsunade is incapacitated, Naruto fights Kabuto in her place and eventually defeats him with a perfected Rasengan, which he formed by using a shadow clone. Naruto collapses almost immediately afterwards, his heart muscles torn by Kabuto, but Tsunade successfully manages to heal him, gives him the necklace he wins, and accepts the position of Hokage. After Orochimaru and Kabuto are defeated, Naruto and the others return to Konoha, Tsunade confidently believing Naruto would someday become a great Hokage.

### Land of Tea Escort Mission

Main article: [Land of Tea Escort Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Tea_Escort_Mission "Land of Tea Escort Mission")

![Naruto carrying Idate.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/9/96/Run_Idate_Run%21_Nagi_Island_Awaits%21.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150220134652)

Naruto carrying Idate.

After Sasuke is healed by Tsunade, Team 7 is assigned the mission of escorting a racer from the [Land of Tea](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Tea "Land of Tea") without Kakashi. Along the way, they meet an arrogant boy named [Idate](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Idate_Morino "Idate Morino") who openly shows his hatred towards shinobi. He also shows off his impressive speed by running away from Team 7 after conning them into paying his bill at a restaurant. Upon arriving in the Land of Tea and meeting the [Wasabi family](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wasabi_family "Wasabi family") 's leader, [Jirōchō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jir%C5%8Dch%C5%8D "Jirōchō"), Naruto and his teammates are shocked to see Idate again and even more so as they learn that he is the client they're supposed to escort. During the race, Naruto and Idate continue to show their dislike of each other. He also learns that Idate is the younger brother of [Ibiki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ibiki_Morino "Ibiki Morino"), and they also come across the [three Ame genin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Oboro "Team Oboro") from the [Chūnin Exams](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%ABnin_Exams "Chūnin Exams"). After defeating them, Team 7 and Idate run into [Aoi Rokushō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Aoi_Rokush%C5%8D "Aoi Rokushō"), a former Konoha [chūnin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ch%C5%ABnin "Chūnin") who betrayed Konoha to join [Amegakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Amegakure "Amegakure"). He was also originally Idate's teacher and the one responsible for Idate running away from Konoha. After barely surviving Aoi’s attack, Idate explains how after he was failed by Ibiki himself from the Chūnin Exams, Aoi tricked Idate into stealing a special scroll and the [Sword of the Thunder God](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sword_of_the_Thunder_God "Sword of the Thunder God") from the village. Being able to relate with Idate in both being [taken advantage of](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mizuki "Mizuki") and the need to [have others](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke "Sasuke") recognise them, Naruto helps Idate regain his self-worth and continue the race.

![Naruto clashes against Aoi.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/ef/Naruto_vs._Aoi.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20260208013659)

Naruto clashes against Aoi.

While managing to catch up in the race, Aoi confronts Team 7 and Idate once again, and uses the Thunder Sword to initially overpower the Team 7. But thanks to Sasuke fighting Aoi and weakening the sword with his [Chidori](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chidori "Chidori"), Naruto is able to destroy it when they clash, and defeats Aoi with his [Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rasengan "Rasengan"). With the danger now passed, Naruto helps Idate catch up to [Fukusuke Hikyakuya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fukusuke_Hikyakuya "Fukusuke Hikyakuya"), the other racer. Though Fukusuke is already near the finish line, with determination and encouragement from Naruto, Idate manages to catch up and win the race, seemingly saving the Wasabi family in the nick of time. However, the [Tea Daimyō's minister](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Evil_Minister_of_the_Tea_Daimy%C5%8D "Evil Minister of the Tea Daimyō") objects, claiming that Idate cheated as Naruto carried him for part of the race. According to the minister, this violates the rule that the runners must do the race on their own two legs. Just as it seems that Idate will be disqualified and the Wasabi family will be disbanded forever, the [Tea Daimyō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tea_Daimy%C5%8D "Tea Daimyō") himself objects, declaring that no such rule exists. He then reveals a photo of the minister taking a bribe from the [Wagarashi family](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wagarashi_family "Wagarashi family"), explaining why he had invented the rule. As a result of this, as well as other poor conduct, the Wagarashi are forcibly disbanded for all time. Afterwards, Team 7 return to Konoha in order to send Sasuke to the hospital for the injuries he suffered during his battle with Aoi.

### Sasuke Recovery Mission

Main article: [Sasuke Recovery Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Recovery_Mission "Sasuke Recovery Mission")

![Naruto promises Sakura to return Sasuke to Konoha.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/4/42/Naruto%27s_Promise.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150224121926)

Naruto promises Sakura to return Sasuke to Konoha.

Naruto visits a recovering Sasuke at the hospital, but is immediately challenged to a fight, to which Naruto eventually agrees. The fight escalates quickly, culminating with Naruto using Rasengan and Sasuke using Chidori. Kakashi arrives and deflects their attacks into opposing water towers before they can clash, Naruto unknowingly doing more damage than Sasuke. Distressed by Sasuke's behaviour, Sakura informs Naruto of the cursed seal Sasuke received from Orochimaru, though Naruto assures Sakura that Sasuke would never abandon Konoha for power. Unfortunately, Sasuke does just that and Naruto joins the [Sasuke Recovery Team](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Recovery_Team "Sasuke Recovery Team") in order to bring him back; he promises a tearful Sakura to do so before he leaves. En route to Sasuke, the team encounters members of the [Sound Four](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sound_Four "Sound Four"), each of which battles a member of the recovery team, while Naruto engages [Kimimaro](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kimimaro "Kimimaro"). Overwhelmed by Kimimaro's taijutsu skills, Naruto is saved at the last minute by Rock Lee, who volunteers to fight Kimimaro while Naruto heads after Sasuke.

![Naruto fights Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/e6/Naruto_Sasuke_clash.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222045618)

Naruto fights Sasuke.

Naruto finally meets Sasuke at the [Valley of the End](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Valley_of_the_End "Valley of the End"). His pleas for Sasuke to come back to Konoha and warns him that Orochimaru will [take his body](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Living_Corpse_Reincarnation "Living Corpse Reincarnation"), but his pleadings fall on deaf ears. Naruto starts attacking him, ready to take him back to Konoha by force if necessary. Undeterred, Sasuke responds by tapping into his cursed seal and landing a series of heavy blows on Naruto. It becomes painfully clear to Naruto that Sasuke is fighting with an intent to kill, so Naruto utilises the Nine-Tails' chakra to overpower Sasuke. Naruto says that Sasuke is like a brother to him and that he will do anything to protect that bond. Sasuke vows to sever that bond, but acknowledges Naruto as an equal by putting on his forehead protector. They continue trading blows, with Naruto eventually manifesting a [fox-shaped cloak](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms#Version_1 "Jinchūriki Forms") and Sasuke entering his [cursed seal](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Cursed_Seal_of_Heaven "Cursed Seal of Heaven") 's second level. Naruto clashes his Rasengan with Sasuke's Chidori, and within the dome of resulting energy they trade final blows: Sasuke punches Naruto and Naruto scratches Sasuke's forehead protector.

![Naruto defeated by Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/b6/Sasuke_defeats_Naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150221070005)

Naruto defeated by Sasuke.

When the energy dissipates, Naruto lies defeated, but Sasuke spares him and continues his way to Orochimaru, leaving his scratched forehead protector behind. Kakashi and [Pakkun](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Pakkun "Pakkun") arrive late and failing to retrieve Sasuke, leave with Naruto. In the hospital, Naruto once again promises Sakura to bring Sasuke back one day. Soon after, Jiraiya arrived with an offer to train Naruto for preparation against Orochimaru and Akatsuki in three years and to give up on Sasuke, as he is no different from Orochimaru. Naruto accepts Jiraiya's training, but refuses to give up on Sasuke, satisfying Jiraiya.

### Original Anime Arcs

*See also: [Plot of Naruto](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Plot_of_Naruto "Plot of Naruto")*

![Naruto leaving Konoha.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/ca/Naruto_Departing_Konoha.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20230224022502)

Naruto leaving Konoha.

As in the manga, Naruto later departs with Jiraiya to begin his two and a half years of training, after making a determined gesture at the Fourth Hokage's statue. In the anime, this does not happen until about 3 months later as Jiraiya was occupied with important intel gathering about Akatsuki for Konohagakure. During the waiting period, Tsunade gives Naruto several missions with members of the [Konoha 11](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_11 "Konoha 11") to keep him busy.

![Naruto training with the Big Ball Rasengan.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/06/Naruto_Improving_Resengan.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20250919130912)

Naruto training with the Big Ball Rasengan.

In the anime, shortly after leaving Konoha, Jiraiya and Naruto discuss the fact that Naruto will need to learn how to counter genjutsu if he hopes to be a match against Sasuke the next time they meet. Naruto practises with [Gamariki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamariki "Gamariki") to dispel genjutsu, but he struggles with it and his chakra keeps hitting Gamariki, who doesn't appreciate it. Naruto follows Jiraiya to the [Genjutsu Tree Village](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genjutsu_Tree_Village "Genjutsu Tree Village") to try a different approach, but they discover the village has been taken over by [Kandachi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kandachi "Kandachi").[^151] They free the villagers and then attack Kandachi, who Naruto eventually defeats with his newly-created [Big Ball Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Ball_Rasengan "Big Ball Rasengan"). As they leave afterwards, Jiraiya is pleased by how similar Naruto is to [Minato Namikaze](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato_Namikaze "Minato Namikaze") and hugs him, which Naruto is bothered by.[^152]

### In Naruto's Footsteps: The Friends' Paths

Main article: [In Naruto's Footsteps: The Friends' Paths](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/In_Naruto%27s_Footsteps:_The_Friends%27_Paths "In Naruto's Footsteps: The Friends' Paths") Two years into their training, Naruto's control of the Nine-Tails' chakra has improved significantly. Jiraiya tries to give him more access to the Nine-Tails' power and uses [Gerotora](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gerotora "Gerotora") to weaken Naruto's [Eight Trigrams Sealing Style](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Trigrams_Sealing_Style "Eight Trigrams Sealing Style"). Seizing the opportunity, the Nine-Tails mocked Naruto for his inability to save Sasuke, using Naruto's negative emotions to force him to enter a [version 2](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Version_2 "Version 2") form with four tails. Not in control of his body, Naruto attacks Jiraiya and nearly kills him before he manages to suppress the Nine-Tails. Naruto has no memory of what happened and Jiraiya doesn't tell him, instead, shifting Naruto's training to other pursuits, including keeping his anger in check so that the Nine-Tails won't flare up again.[^153]

## Part II

*See also: [Plot of Naruto: Shippūden](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Plot_of_Naruto:_Shipp%C5%ABden "Plot of Naruto: Shippūden")*

### Kazekage Rescue Mission

Main article: [Kazekage Rescue Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kazekage_Rescue_Mission "Kazekage Rescue Mission")

![Naruto returns to Konohagakure.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/89/Naruto_returns_to_Konoha.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221004113050)

Naruto returns to Konohagakure.

Naruto and Jiraiya return to Konoha after two-and-a-half years of training, where Naruto becomes surprised by Tsunade's newly-formed statue on [Hokage Rock](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage_Rock "Hokage Rock"). He greets his old friends afterwards: he gives Kakashi a copy of *[Icha Icha Tactics](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Icha_Icha_Tactics "Icha Icha Tactics")* as a gift; he catches up with Sakura, whom he has become taller than; he gets back into a competition of [Sexy Techniques](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy_Technique "Sexy Technique") with [Konohamaru Sarutobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konohamaru_Sarutobi "Konohamaru Sarutobi"), only to be violently reprimanded by Sakura. Kakashi reforms Team 7 with them and gives them another [bell test](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Bell_test "Bell test"), but unlike last time, taking the bells from him is the real objective. As with last time, Naruto launches a first attack before the test officially begins, but this time it nearly succeeds. Despite their improved abilities, Naruto and Sakura were still unable to get a bell through conventional means, and it was only by Naruto's threat to spoil the latest *[Icha Icha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Icha_Icha "Icha Icha")* novel that they distracted Kakashi long enough to take the bells.

While Team 7 tries without success to find a mission to go on that Naruto won't complain about, word reaches Konoha that [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki") has kidnapped [Gaara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gaara "Gaara"), the [Kazekage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kazekage "Kazekage"). Team 7 is sent to Sunagakure to assist in rescuing Gaara. On their way to Suna, Naruto explains that Gaara was kidnapped because he is the [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki") of the [One-Tail](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/One-Tail "One-Tail"), just as Naruto is the jinchūriki of the [Nine-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails "Nine-Tails"). Upon arrival in Suna, Naruto defends Kakashi from [Chiyo](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chiyo "Chiyo") 's assault when she mistakes him for his father. Team 7 gathers what intel they can on Gaara's kidnappers and leave to go after them; Chiyo volunteers to escort them since they aren't familiar with the country around Suna. Naruto promises a recovering [Kankurō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kankur%C5%8D "Kankurō") to rescue Gaara before they leave.

![Naruto pummels Deidara.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/2/25/Naruto_pummels_Deidara.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20230423165954)

Naruto pummels Deidara.

While en route to an Akatsuki lair, they are confronted by [Itachi Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Itachi_Uchiha "Itachi Uchiha"), who traps Naruto in a genjutsu. Sakura and Chiyo release him, allowing Naruto to team up with Kakashi and defeat Itachi with a [Big Ball Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Big_Ball_Rasengan "Big Ball Rasengan"). The Itachi is discovered to be an [impostor](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shapeshifting_Technique "Shapeshifting Technique"), so they continue to the Akatsuki lair, meeting up with [Team Guy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Guy "Team Guy"), who takes down the barrier over the entrance so that Team 7 can get in. They find Gaara's body finds with his kidnappers, [Deidara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Deidara "Deidara") and [Sasori](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasori "Sasori"). Deidara flies off with Gaara's body with Naruto and Kakashi in pursuit, leaving Sasori to Chiyo and Sakura. Naruto, enraged, makes repeated failed attempts to rescue Gaara before he is calmed by Kakashi, who uses his new [Mangekyō Sharingan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mangeky%C5%8D_Sharingan "Mangekyō Sharingan") to distract Deidara with [Kamui](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kamui "Kamui"). Naruto retrieves Gaara's body, but seeing it drives him over the edge: he furiously attacks Deidara and beats him mercilessly into the ground. Discovering that it was a [Clay Clone](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Clay_Clone "Clay Clone"), Naruto slips into his [two-tailed](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Version_1 "Version 1") form in rage, but is restored to normal by Kakashi via the [Chakra-Suppressing Seal](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chakra-Suppressing_Seal "Chakra-Suppressing Seal").

![Gaara awakens, with Naruto by his side.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/05/Gaara_awakens.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150221103627)

Gaara awakens, with Naruto by his side.

Deidara escapes, while Naruto and Kakashi regroup with Sakura, Chiyo, and Team Guy. Sakura attempts to revive Gaara, but the [removal](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sealing_Technique:_Phantom_Dragons_Nine_Consuming_Seals "Sealing Technique: Phantom Dragons Nine Consuming Seals") of Shukaku has caused him to die. Naruto breaks down in tears and angrily lashes out at Chiyo, saying she had no right to make Gaara not only a jinchūriki, but also lose his life because of it. As atonement and with Naruto's aid, Chiyo sacrifices her life to revive Gaara; as the jutsu nears completion, Chiyo voices her faith in Naruto's ability to save Gaara and to become Hokage. Naruto greets Gaara when he awakens, and a few days later, Teams 7 and Guy attend Chiyo's funeral in Suna. Before returning home, Naruto and Gaara shake hands (Gaara using his sand) as a sign of their close relationship.

### Tenchi Bridge Reconnaissance Mission

Main article: [Tenchi Bridge Reconnaissance Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tenchi_Bridge_Reconnaissance_Mission "Tenchi Bridge Reconnaissance Mission")

![Naruto and Sai's first encounter.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/6/61/Naruto_and_Sai%27s_first_meeting.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221003164058)

Naruto and Sai's first encounter.

During her fight with Sasori, Sakura learned of an opportunity to meet with a spy in Orochimaru's ranks in a few days' time, hoping it will lead them to Sasuke. Kakashi is left bedridden from his fight with Deidara, so [Yamato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yamato "Yamato") leads Team 7 as his replacement. Replacing Sasuke on the team is [Sai](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sai "Sai"), whom Naruto actually encounters earlier and dislikes, declaring him an inferior version of Sasuke. Sai is happy for this distinction and proceeds to degrade Sasuke for defecting from Konoha, forcing Yamato to use his [Wood Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wood_Release "Wood Release") to break up their fight. Naruto is constantly exasperated by Sai's lack of empathy and his ridicule of Sasuke, but resolves to work with Sai if it means saving Sasuke.

![Team 7 confronts Orochimaru and Kabuto.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/04/Team_Yamato_at_Tenchi_Bridge.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221003120655)

Team 7 confronts Orochimaru and Kabuto.

Yamato disguises himself as Sasori and goes to the [Tenchi Bridge](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tenchi_Bridge "Tenchi Bridge") to meet the spy while Naruto, Sakura, and Sai hide nearby. The spy, [Kabuto Yakushi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kabuto_Yakushi "Kabuto Yakushi"), begins telling Yamato about Orochimaru's organisation, but they are interrupted by the arrival of Orochimaru, who teams up with Kabuto to fight Yamato, having intended to kill Sasori. Team 7 comes to his aid and Orochimaru, recognising them, taunts Naruto about Sasuke. Naruto is enraged and strikes him, using his [version 1](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Version_1 "Version 1") form to make his attacks more devastating. Naruto's rage intensifies as he submits to the Nine-Tails' influence: he destroys the Tenchi Bridge and eventually advances to his [version 2](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Version_2 "Version 2") form while fighting Orochimaru. Naruto is soon forced back to the destroyed bridge, where, unable to tell friend from foe, he unknowingly attacks Sakura when she approaches him.

![A four-tailed Naruto attacks Orochimaru with Tailed Beast Ball.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/0b/Naruto_vs._Orochimaru.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20231125032008)

A four-tailed Naruto attacks Orochimaru with Tailed Beast Ball.

Yamato restrains Naruto with his Wood Release and suppresses the Nine-Tails' influence, but its chakra leaves Naruto's body badly damaged. After Sakura heals him, Naruto wakes up unable to remember what happened, but is surprised by the devastated landscape. When they realise that Sai is missing, Yamato reports that he has joined with Orochimaru and Kabuto. On the way, Yamato secretly takes Naruto aside and tells him that he is the one who attacked Sakura. He encourages Naruto to use his own strength instead of the Nine-Tails in order to protect his loved ones. The team locates Sai, whom Yamato has placed a [trace](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Transmission_Wood "Transmission Wood") on, at Orochimaru's lair and capture him. Restrained, Sai asks why Naruto is determined to save him; Naruto replies that his bond with Sasuke is too precious to be broken and that he will do anything to protect it. Intrigued, Sai switches sides, helping them capture Kabuto and then searching the base for Sasuke on Naruto's behalf. While he's gone, Yamato goes through his belongings and finds evidence that Sai has been assigned to assassinate Sasuke.

![Naruto encounters Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/5/59/Sasuke_draws_sword.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150202074839)

Naruto encounters Sasuke.

When they find Sai, he explains that he truly does want to help retrieve Sasuke, and in fact has already found him: Naruto and Sakura are speechless to see Sasuke again. Sasuke reacts with indifference to them, but Naruto remarks he can't become Hokage without saving his friend. To demonstrate that they mean nothing to him, Sasuke quickly neutralises them all. As Naruto struggles to avoid the temptation of using the Nine-Tails, Sasuke suddenly appears in Naruto's subconscious using his Sharingan and suppresses the Nine-Tails. He then prepares to kill them, but is persuaded not to by Orochimaru and leaves without further comment. Naruto is upset at having failed once more to bring Sasuke home, but is reminded by Sakura that they need to become stronger and Team 7 returns to Konoha.

### Twelve Guardian Ninja

Main article: [Twelve Guardian Ninja (Arc)](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Twelve_Guardian_Ninja_\(Arc\) "Twelve Guardian Ninja (Arc)")

![Naruto and Sora meet for the first time.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/0/05/Naruto_and_Sora_meet.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210905005126)

Naruto and Sora meet for the first time.

In the anime, Team Kakashi, with Yamato still in command, is sent on a mission to the [Fire Temple](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fire_Temple "Fire Temple"). There, they met [Sora](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sora "Sora"), a young [monk](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ninja_monk "Ninja monk") who openly expresses his dislike of Naruto, with the two bickering at every opportunity. Despite this, Sora is added to their team to help investigate the grave robberies of several former members of the [Twelve Guardian Ninja](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Twelve_Guardian_Ninja "Twelve Guardian Ninja"). Shortly after the investigation begins, Naruto learns that the other monks have long treated Sora much like the villagers of Konoha treated him: with contempt, hatred, and keeping their distance from him. Naruto questions him about it, but Sora simply replies that he doesn't blame them.

Upon encountering the grave robbers, Naruto is forced to fight [Fūka](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/F%C5%ABka "Fūka"), a woman who, much to his shock, repeatedly attempts to kiss him. Through the use of the Shadow Clone Technique, he is able to evade her advances, only to discover that she is a master of all five [nature transformations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nature_Transformation "Nature Transformation"), a feat that was unheard of by then. Despite his best efforts, however, Fūka ultimately manages to [kiss](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Execution_by_Kiss "Execution by Kiss") him, an act that drains all the chakra from his body. Once Naruto's chakra is drained, however, Fūka begins to siphon the Nine-Tails' chakra, a force she can't contain and she forces herself off him. She then retreats with her team, and Team Kakashi returns to Konoha, with Sora as a temporary replacement for Sai.

Naruto continues to try to befriend Sora, only for his and the efforts of others in Konoha to be rebuffed. Later, Naruto finds Sora, who had just attempted to kill Tsunade and [Asuma Sarutobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Asuma_Sarutobi "Asuma Sarutobi"), and questions him on his newfound murderous hostility, to which Sora bitterly replies that Asuma murdered his [father](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kazuma "Kazuma"). Despite Asuma's attempts to explain the situation, Sora refuses to listen and runs away, with Naruto pursuing him until being stopped by Fūka. Forced to fight her again, Naruto discovers that she apparently regenerates her injuries by switching bodies. Noticing that she has since stopped using certain elements, Naruto realizes she possesses some of Fūka's techniques, and that each body had its own affinity that she can switch between. Concluding that her soul resides in her hair, Naruto destroys all of Fūka's bodies and strikes her hair with Rasengan, killing her, before continuing his pursuit of Sora.

![Naruto (in his Version 1 state) facing Sora (in his Version 2 state).](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/c/c4/Resonance.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210904231055)

Naruto (in his Version 1 state) facing Sora (in his Version 2 state).

Soon thereafter, Naruto learns that not only is Sora's father still alive, but that he was behind several serious thefts and manipulated Sora into attempting to kill Tsunade. The most pressing matter, however, is that he had, years earlier, sealed some of Nine-Tails' chakra, having gathered it after the Tailed Beast's attack on the village, into Sora and attempts to use its power to destroy Konoha. When Sora loses control of the Nine-Tails' chakra within him, Naruto also begins to struggle to control the Nine-Tails, but is ultimately able to hold the Tailed Beast's influence back, and is able to reach Sora and help him return to normal. Afterwards, Nine-Tails' chakra is expelled from Sora's body, and with the situation finally over, Sora departs to travel the world.

### Akatsuki Suppression Mission

Main article: [Akatsuki Suppression Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki_Suppression_Mission "Akatsuki Suppression Mission")

![Naruto training with his clones.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/de/Naruto_training.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20231125032148)

Naruto training with his clones.

Once Kakashi is done recuperating in the hospital, he assembles Naruto, Sakura, and Sai to discuss their failed mission to retrieve Sasuke. Kakashi believes the best way to match Sasuke is for Naruto to create a new [jutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jutsu "Jutsu"). As the training began, Naruto discovers that his [nature](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nature "Nature") is [wind](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind "Wind") and learns to use the wind nature. Ordinarily, such training would take months or years, but Kakashi advises that Naruto, by training alongside hundreds of [shadow clones](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_clones "Shadow clones") can do the same training in a mere fraction of the time. With Kakashi's instructions and the training grounds created by Yamato, Naruto quickly masters how to use the wind nature. The next step is more difficult for him: combining that nature with the [Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rasengan "Rasengan").

![Naruto defeats Kakuzu.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/ee/Naruto_vs_Kakuzu_.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20151004005728)

Naruto defeats Kakuzu.

Like Kakashi and [Minato Namikaze](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato_Namikaze "Minato Namikaze") before him, Naruto fails several times in trying to combine his nature with the Rasengan; his frustration evokes the Nine-Tails' chakra at times, which Yamato suppresses. With further advice from Kakashi, Naruto finds his solution using two shadow clones: one helps him form the Rasengan and the other adds his wind nature. When they receive news that Asuma died in battle with members of [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki"), they take a break to attend Asuma's funeral. Kakashi leaves Yamato in charge of overseeing Naruto's training so he could help [Team 10](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Asuma "Team Asuma") avenge Asuma. Having finished his jutsu, Naruto and Team Yamato went to provide assistance, arriving in time to save Kakashi and Team 10 from [Kakuzu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakuzu "Kakuzu"). Naruto engages Kakuzu alone with his newly-created [Wind Release: Rasenshuriken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Rasenshuriken "Wind Release: Rasenshuriken"), though it dissipates on its first use and Naruto is rescued by Kakashi and Yamato. The technique succeeds the second time, destroying two of Kakuzu's hearts and putting the last one on the brink of failure. Kakashi finishes off Kakuzu and they return to Konoha.

The Rasenshuriken injures Naruto's arm after use and he is forbidden to use the technique ever again. When they visit Ichiraku Ramen, Naruto has a hard time eating while his arm mends, so Sakura opts to help, before the responsibility falls to Sai, then to Kakashi. As they leave afterwards, they are met by Konohamaru, who demonstrates his [Sexy: Girl on Girl Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy:_Girl_on_Girl_Technique "Sexy: Girl on Girl Technique"); Naruto approves but Sakura is disgusted and violently reprimands him. In the manga, Konohamaru responds with [Sexy: Boy on Boy Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy:_Boy_on_Boy_Technique "Sexy: Boy on Boy Technique"); Sakura approves but Naruto is disgusted and violently reprimands him.

### Three-Tails' Appearance

Main article: [Three-Tails' Appearance](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Three-Tails%27_Appearance "Three-Tails' Appearance")

![Jiraiya demonstrates cooperation ninjutsu to Naruto.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d2/Gamariki_-_Waterpistol.PNG/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20170816205210)

Jiraiya demonstrates cooperation ninjutsu to Naruto.

In the anime, while Naruto is still recovering from overusing his Rasenshuriken, Jiraiya appears to congratulate his student on his latest achievements: defeating one of the Akatsuki members and completing Minato's jutsu. Jiraiya offers to take him on another training trip, which Naruto quickly agrees to. In fact, the main reason for Naruto's withdrawal from Konoha is again so he couldn't join Kakashi and [Team 8](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_8 "Team 8") on their mission to find Orochimaru's last hiding place. For their training, they head to a nearby town to teach Naruto how to use [cooperation ninjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Cooperation_ninjutsu "Cooperation ninjutsu") with the toads. [Gamariki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamariki "Gamariki") is initially summoned to help in this endeavor, but when Naruto proves unable to work with him, [Gamakichi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamakichi "Gamakichi") and [Gamatatsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamatatsu "Gamatatsu") are summoned instead.

Later, while resting in one of the hot springs, Naruto encounters a young boy named [Yūkimaru](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Y%C5%ABkimaru "Yūkimaru"). They briefly talk about bonds, which makes Yūkimaru happy. When Yūkimaru left, Naruto continues his training and begins to succeed in synchronized jutsu with Gamakichi and Gamatatsu. Jiraiya is soon summoned, so they return to Konoha where Gamatatsu and Naruto learn how to perform a [Water Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Water_Release "Water Release") -based technique together. Soon after, Naruto meets up with Sai, who accidentally lets it slip that the rest of the team are about to assist Kakashi and Team 8 in their search for Orochimaru. Determined to help in order to find Sasuke, Naruto convinces Tsunade to let him go as well. While searching for Team 8, Naruto finds Yūkimaru again and offers him the opportunity to accompany him back to Konoha, to which Yūkimaru responds by quickly disappearing. When Team Yamato is stopped by [Guren](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Guren "Guren") 's [crystal barrier](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Crystal_Release:_Jade_Crystal_Labyrinth_Technique "Crystal Release: Jade Crystal Labyrinth Technique"), Naruto summons Gamakichi and Gamatatsu to help and together, they use [Wind Release: Toad Gun](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Wind_Release:_Toad_Gun "Wind Release: Toad Gun") to break through the barrier, and on the opposite side, they encounter Team 8. When they realize Hinata has been captured, they help rescue her before retreating. The teams follow Guren to a lake, where Naruto is sent ahead to scout the area. He finds Guren and [her companions](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Team_Guren "Team Guren") (including Yūkimaru) along with the [Three-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Three-Tails "Three-Tails"), which creates a wave that carries them all back to shore.

![Naruto prepares to jump with an embarrassed Guren.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/89/Naruto_lifts_Guren.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20151030203214)

Naruto prepares to jump with an embarrassed Guren.

While the teams decide what to do with the Three-Tails, Naruto goes off to find Yūkimaru and ends up running into Guren once again. They discuss what would truly make Yūkimaru happy, who soon reveals himself to be fine who despite being appreciate of Naruto's efforts, decides to stay by Guren's side. Before he can pursue them, Naruto is called back to help with deal with the Three-Tails, with his goal being to prevent Guren and her group from interfering with its sealing. Despite his best efforts, Guren is able to stop the sealing and Yūkimaru uses this as an opportunity to try to control the Tailed Beast, causing both Naruto and Guren to worry. In the process, the two are swallowed by the Three-Tails, giving them the opportunity to talk and discover what both of their interests in Yūkimaru are. Putting their differences aside, Naruto and Guren team up to escape. Once outside, they find [Rinji](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rinji "Rinji") giving Yūkimaru some pills, and after they manage to stop Rinji, they learn that he is actually Kabuto in disguise. Kabuto reveals that Rinji had long ago become part of his corpse collection and is promptly [reanimated](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Dead_Soul_Technique "Dead Soul Technique") with his medical ninjutsu.

While Naruto faces Kabuto, the resurrected Rinji fights Guren, with the former's ultrasonic waves being more than enough to break Guren's [Crystal Release](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Crystal_Release "Crystal Release"), making it useless against Rinji. After Guren crystallises herself to defeat Rinji, Yūkimaru's grief causes him to let loose his power, driving the Three-Tails into a frenzy, and shattering the seal the Konoha ninja were very close to completing. Later, when the Three-Tails escaped and Kabuto disappeared, Naruto goes to help the weakened Yūkimaru. Later, Shizune is able to heal Yūkimaru, but explains that his chakra network is too damaged and that he'll never be able to control the Three-Tails again. The next morning, after Tsunade ordered the team to return to Konoha, Naruto discovers that Yūkimaru has disappeared and after seeing that he had left Guren's crystal behind, Naruto realizes that Guren is still alive and that she's with Yūkimaru. Knowing that the two are well together, Naruto decides not to pursue them and keeps their survival a secret from the others.

### Itachi Pursuit Mission

Main article: [Itachi Pursuit Mission](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Itachi_Pursuit_Mission "Itachi Pursuit Mission")

![The Eight Man Squad formed to pursue Itachi and Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/7/75/Eight_Man_Squad.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210806023638)

The Eight Man Squad formed to pursue Itachi and Sasuke.

News reaches Konoha that Sasuke has killed Orochimaru. Realising that this is a good opportunity to try once again to reunite with Sasuke, Kakashi combines Teams 7 and 8 into an [Eight Man Squad](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Man_Squad "Eight Man Squad") with the mission to find either Sasuke or his assumed target, Itachi. When they split up to search, Naruto, because he's a target of Akatsuki, is given a protective escort in the form of Hinata, Yamato, and [Bull](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Bull "Bull"). Their group encounters Kabuto, who offers them intel on Akatsuki and its members, his thanks to Naruto "inspiring" him to overcome Orochimaru after absorbing his remains. Kabuto then escapes.

### Tale of Jiraiya the Gallant

Main article: [Tale of Jiraiya the Gallant](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tale_of_Jiraiya_the_Gallant "Tale of Jiraiya the Gallant")

![Naruto and Itachi converse.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/a0/Itachi%27s_Genjutsu.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150221103240)

Naruto and Itachi converse.

After regrouping and [Kiba Inuzuka](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kiba_Inuzuka "Kiba Inuzuka") detects Sasuke's trail, Naruto creates shadow clones to search the area faster, one of which encounters Itachi. Itachi repels Naruto's attacks and insists he only wants to talk. He asks Naruto what Sasuke means to him and what he will do if Sasuke ever moves against Konoha. Naruto replies that he is Sasuke's brother – a better brother than Itachi is – and that if Sasuke ever attacks the village he will defend it without killing Sasuke. Itachi is happy with this answer and gives Naruto some assistance for this purpose: a [special crow](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Itachi%27s_Crow "Itachi's Crow") that he stores within Naruto's body. Itachi left, and Naruto continued his search, eventually finding Sasuke.[^154]

As they near Sasuke's location they are intercepted by [Tobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tobi "Tobi") of Akatsuki, who prevents them from progressing and who is invulnerable to their attacks.

### Fated Battle Between Brothers

Main article: [Fated Battle Between Brothers](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fated_Battle_Between_Brothers "Fated Battle Between Brothers") As the group continually tries to attack Tobi, he dodges the attacks with ease. Naruto tries several times to attack him with his Rasengan, but without success. Tobi attempts to use a hidden jutsu to attack the teams, but fails at it. Eventually, Zetsu comes to tell him about Sasuke and Itachi's fight. Tobi leaves when he receives news that Sasuke has killed Itachi, and Teams 7 and 8 try to reach Sasuke before he does. Unable to find where Tobi has taken Sasuke, they are forced to return to Konoha.

### Six-Tails Unleashed

Main article: [Six-Tails Unleashed](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six-Tails_Unleashed "Six-Tails Unleashed")

![Naruto battles against Utakata.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/6/62/Utakata_vs_naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150826054806)

Naruto battles against Utakata.

In the anime, after failing to retrieve Sasuke, Naruto's journey back to Konoha is interrupted by [Katsuyu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Katsuyu "Katsuyu"), sent by Tsunade, who sends Team Yamato to help the [Tsuchigumo Clan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsuchigumo_Clan "Tsuchigumo Clan") protect the clan's [kinjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kinjutsu "Kinjutsu"), [Fury](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fury "Fury"). Upon arriving at the Tsuchigumo clan's fortress, they discover the kinjutsu is with the granddaughter of the former clan leader, [En no Gyōja](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/En_no_Gy%C5%8Dja "En no Gyōja"). When Team Yamato locate her, Naruto attacks the shinobi near her, the [Six-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six-Tails "Six-Tails") ' jinchūriki, [Utakata](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Utakata "Utakata"), believing him to be the enemy. Upon seeing the strength of the Konoha ninja, Utakata leaves [Hotaru](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hotaru "Hotaru") in their care and departs. Due to her desire to be taught by Utakata, Hotaru refuses Team Yamato's help, but Yamato has a plan to use Sai's leeching jutsu to frighten Hotaru and force her to accept Team Yamato's help on their journey to [Tsuchigumo Village](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsuchigumo_Village "Tsuchigumo Village"), with Naruto blocking all of Sai's ink-based leeches while walking with Hotaru. When they arrive, Team Yamato leaves Hotaru in the care of the villagers, but Naruto, noticing the way the villagers looked at her, goes back to make sure everything is okay. When he arrives, however, Naruto realizes that Hotaru has been captured by bandits and the villagers haven't even taken care of her, so Naruto and his team set out to find her.

![Naruto attacks a Kiri hunter-nin with Rasengan.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/7/7a/Naruto_vs_Kiri-nin.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20251009224213)

Naruto attacks a Kiri hunter-nin with Rasengan.

When Naruto encounters a group of [Kirigakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kirigakure "Kirigakure") [hunter-nin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hunter-nin "Hunter-nin") holding Hotaru hostage and trying to capture Utakata, Naruto attacks one of the Kiri-nin, freeing Utakata, and attempts to rescue Hotaru. However, before the fighting can escalate further, Yamato and the Kiri [Anbu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Anbu "Anbu") leader, [Tsurugi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsurugi "Tsurugi"), decide that for the duration of Yamato's team's mission, Utakata will stay with Hotaru and Konoha will not interfere with their capture of the [missing-nin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Missing-nin "Missing-nin") afterward, in order to avoid starting an unnecessary war between Konoha and Kiri. Before leaving, Tsurugi tells Naruto that Utakata is wanted for the murder of his master, a revelation that shocks Hotaru. Seeing her uncertainty, Naruto insists that it doesn't matter what personal faults, and tells her how he can relate as he never knew his parents, but has come to respect and look up to Jiraiya despite his questionable antics. Hotaru is relieved to hear this, once again determined to learn under Utakata, but promptly collapses from a fever. While Utakata tends to Hotaru, he instructs Naruto to find the herbs needed to heal her. When he returns with the herbs, Naruto learns that Hotaru had been made a vessel for her clan's kinjutsu, not unlike how he was made the jinchūriki of the Nine-Tails. Naruto is furious to see that Hotaru has been transformed into a living weapon, but she tells him that she had willingly decided to receive the kinjutsu seal to help revive her clan. When they arrive at the Tsuchigumo clan's stronghold, where [Tonbee](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tonbee "Tonbee") was being tended to by Sakura, Tonbee tells her that the Tsuchigumo clan was too weak to ever be restored and believes it best to destroy the kinjutsu, using a special technique developed by her grandfather to prevent Hotaru from being harmed in the event that the kinjutsu had to be destroyed. However, Hotaru still wishes to revive her clan and asks Naruto to find [Shiranami](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shiranami "Shiranami"), who knows how to use her grandfather's kinjutsu.

![Naruto and Utakata confront Shiranami.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/e/eb/Naruto_and_Utakata_vs._Shiranami.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20251009224940)

Naruto and Utakata confront Shiranami.

Sympathizing with Hotaru's desire to follow her master's dreams, Naruto requests two days to find Shiranami. After several failed attempts, Naruto is told by an old man he knows where Shiranami lives. After entering his home, however, Naruto is ambushed by Shiranami's team, who himself is actually the bandit leader. Trapped within a chakra-draining barrier, Naruto is rescued by Utakata, who uses the Six-Tails' chakra to drive the bandits away. They follow Hotaru, who had been captured by Shiranami and taken to the village, where the villagers are being controlled by a jutsu. While Yamato, Sakura, and Sai tend to the villagers, Naruto and Utakata go off to rescue Hotaru. When they arrive, Shiranami uses Hotaru to gather the chakra needed to use Fury, and manages to the two jinchuuriki with his [Character Bind Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Character_Bind_Technique "Character Bind Technique"). However, both Naruto and Utakata use their tailed beast chakra to break the attack and move in to attack, only for Shiranami, who gained control of the kinjutsu, to use Fury. However, the full energy of Fury fails to activate, causing Shiranami to flee. Despite his attempts, Shiranami is defeated after being struck by Naruto's Rasengan. After Shiranami's defeat, the Tsuchigumo clan thanks Team Yamato, who then promptly resume their journey back to Konoha.

### Pain's Assault

Main article: [Pain's Assault](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Pain%27s_Assault_\(Arc\) "Pain's Assault (Arc)")

![Naruto mourns Jiraiya's death.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/da/Naruto_after_Jiraiya%27s_death.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20141201100739)

Naruto mourns Jiraiya's death.

As Naruto contemplated his meeting with Itachi, he was called to the [Hokage Residence](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage_Residence "Hokage Residence") to hear sombre news: his master Jiraiya was killed by [Pain](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Pain "Pain"), the leader of Akatsuki. Grief-stricken, Naruto blamed Tsunade and spent the day mourning his teacher's death, depressed that Jiraiya cannot see him become Hokage. After being comforted by [Iruka](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Iruka "Iruka") and [Shikamaru](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shikamaru "Shikamaru"), Naruto helped to decipher Jiraiya's dying message and eventually they succeed. Understanding that Jiraiya had bought him time for his own fight with Pain, Naruto went to train with the toads of [Mount Myōboku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mount_My%C5%8Dboku "Mount Myōboku") to learn [senjutsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Senjutsu "Senjutsu"). Naruto trained to harmonise with nature, a process he sped up by using a limited number of shadow clones. He rapidly progressed through the training stages and attained a perfect [Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Mode "Sage Mode"), which Jiraiya was unable to do. News of [Pain's Assault](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Pain%27s_Assault "Pain's Assault") on Konoha reached them, they began to mobilise for battle.

![Naruto arrives to fight Pain.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/af/Naruto_and_Fukasaku.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150125203535)

Naruto arrives to fight Pain.

As Naruto, [Fukasaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fukasaku "Fukasaku"), [Gamaken](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamaken "Gamaken"), [Gamahiro](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamahiro "Gamahiro"), [Gamabunta](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamabunta "Gamabunta"), and [Gamakichi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gamakichi "Gamakichi") were summoned to the centre of Konoha, the group was confronted by the [Six Paths of Pain](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Six_Paths_of_Pain "Six Paths of Pain"), who had just destroyed the village. After destroying the [Asura Path](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Asura_Path "Asura Path") before it could attack Tsunade, Naruto told her to make sure everyone left the battle to him. After a brief skirmish with [Animal Path](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Animal_Path "Animal Path"), Naruto and the toads defeated the Animal, [Preta](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Preta "Preta"), and [Human Paths](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Human_Path "Human Path") before running out of senjutsu chakra. Naruto revealed that he had two shadow clones waiting back at [Mount Myōboku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Mount_My%C5%8Dboku "Mount Myōboku") to revitalise him with natural energy. Naruto went back on the offensive, dispatching the healed Preta and Naraka Path before the Deva Path regained its full power. Pain killed Fukasaku and used the opportunity to capture Naruto, pinning him down to the ground.

![Naruto transforms into his six-tailed form out of anger.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/4/48/Naruto%27s_Version_2_form.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221003124824)

Naruto transforms into his six-tailed form out of anger.

With Naruto restrained, Pain opened up his reasons for Akatsuki, their plan for the tailed beasts, and the peaceful world he wanted to create. Though Naruto rejected his notion that using a weapon to force peace, he could offer no alternative. Before Pain could depart with Naruto, he was attacked by [Hinata Hyūga](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hinata_Hy%C5%ABga "Hinata Hyūga"). Declaring her love for Naruto and vowing to protect him, she continued to fight against Pain before she was subdued and critically wounded. Believing Hinata to had been killed, an enraged Naruto erupted into his [six-tailed form](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Version_2 "Version 2"). Resisting the [First Hokage's Necklace](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/First_Hokage%27s_Necklace "First Hokage's Necklace") 's attempted to quell the transformation and destroying it, the Nine-Tails attacked Pain, forcing him out of the village to get close enough to his actual body to use the [Chibaku Tensei](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Chibaku_Tensei "Chibaku Tensei"), trapping Naruto within the small satellite. The technique was not enough to stop the Nine-Tails, however, as it simply progressed to [eight-tailed form](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki_Forms#Partial_Transformation "Jinchūriki Forms") to force its way out of the satellite. Meanwhile within his subconscious, Naruto was tempted by the Nine-Tails to open its seal to save him from the pain of not having Pain's answer for peace. However, before Naruto could, [Minato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato "Minato") appeared, having left some of his chakra within the seal in case of an emergency to protect his son. Overjoyed at meeting his father, but angered that he would condemn him to life as a jinchūriki, Naruto listened to his father encouraging him, confident that he would find a way to break the cycle of hatred. After repairing the seal, Minato disappeared and the newly-inspired Naruto prepared to resume his battle with Pain.

![Naruto defeats Pain.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/7/79/Naruto_defeating_Deva_path.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222045336)

Naruto defeats Pain.

Confidence restored, Naruto was greatly relieved to learn that not only had Hinata survived, but nobody was injured during his rampage. After clever use of shadow clones, Naruto destroyed the Deva Path with a Rasengan, defeating the last of Pain's Six Paths. Using one of Pain's [Black Receivers](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Black_Receiver "Black Receiver"), Naruto followed the chakra signal to [Nagato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nagato "Nagato") and [Konan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konan "Konan") 's position. Confronting Nagato, Naruto listened to Nagato's story that turned him into Pain and came to understand why he made the actions he did. Despite not forgiving him, he told Nagato he would not kill him and would instead try to create the better world that their teacher wished for. Moved by Naruto's determination to create a better world, Nagato decided to put his trust in Naruto and sacrificed his own life to [revive](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Outer_Path:_Samsara_of_Heavenly_Life_Technique "Outer Path: Samsara of Heavenly Life Technique") all the people he had killed in Konoha. Naruto helped Konan retrieve Nagato and [Yahiko](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yahiko "Yahiko") 's bodies for burial in [Amegakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Amegakure "Amegakure"), and Konan left Naruto a bouquet of paper flowers for him, symbolising their new alliance.

![The village greets Naruto upon his return.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/d3/Hero_of_the_Hidden_Leaf.PNG/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222030908)

The village greets Naruto upon his return.

Naruto then created a [memorial](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya%27s_Memorial "Jiraiya's Memorial") for [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya") and left the flowers and a copy of *[The Tale of the Utterly Gutsy Shinobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Tale_of_the_Utterly_Gutsy_Shinobi "The Tale of the Utterly Gutsy Shinobi")* by a rock with the kanji for *teacher* (師, *shi*). While the exhausted Naruto walked back to the village, he was found by Kakashi who carried him the rest of the way. Upon arrival, Naruto was greeted as a hero by the villagers, his dream of acknowledgement being realised at last.

### Past Arc: The Locus of Konoha

Main article: [Past Arc: The Locus of Konoha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Past_Arc:_The_Locus_of_Konoha "Past Arc: The Locus of Konoha")

### Five Kage Summit

Main article: [Five Kage Summit](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Five_Kage_Summit_\(Arc\) "Five Kage Summit (Arc)")

![Naruto willingly gets beaten by Karui.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/f/fa/Karui_Beating_Naruto.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20180428081333)

Naruto willingly gets beaten by Karui.

Sakura informs Naruto that Tsunade fell into a coma and that there's nothing that can be done to bring her out of it. While they talk, they are approached by [Tazuna](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tazuna "Tazuna") and [Inari](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Inari "Inari"), who have come to help rebuild Konoha. They ask about Sasuke, which Naruto avoids going into detail about so as to spare them and Sakura a discussion about Sasuke's defection. After Tazuna and Inari leave, they receive news that [Danzō Shimura](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Danz%C5%8D_Shimura "Danzō Shimura") has become the next [Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage "Hokage") and that he has ordered Sasuke be killed as a traitor. Naruto and Sakura approach Sai to ask him how they can convince Danzō to change his mind, but Sai is unable to help. [Omoi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Omoi "Omoi") and [Karui](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Karui "Karui") of [Kumogakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kumogakure "Kumogakure") overhear them talking about Sasuke and they ask for information about Sasuke, wishing to kill him for his role in Akatsuki's capture of [Killer B](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Killer_B "Killer B"). Naruto leads Omoi and Karui away to spare Sakura pain, but refuses to reveal anything about Sasuke. Instead, Naruto allows the Kumo-nin vent their anger by beating him, to which [Karui](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Karui "Karui") obliges until Sai stops her and the Kumo-nin retreat.

![Naruto unsuccessfully pleas the Raikage to forgive Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/6/6e/Naruto%27s_failed_plea_to_Raikage.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20230322222828)

Naruto unsuccessfully pleas the Raikage to forgive Sasuke.

Recovering later, Naruto asked Yamato and Kakashi to take him to the [Land of Iron](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Iron "Land of Iron"), so that he could ask [Fourth Raikage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Raikage "Fourth Raikage") to pardon Sasuke. Upon arrival, the Raikage rejected Naruto's request even with Kakashi and Yamato's assistance and berated him for defending a criminal. Naruto went to a local inn to ponder his next course of action, but was soon confronted by [Tobi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tobi "Tobi"), who wanted to understand Nagato's change of heart. Naruto ignored the question and demanded to know about his plans with Sasuke. Tobi told him about the [Sage of the Six Paths](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_of_the_Six_Paths "Sage of the Six Paths"), the Uchiha clan, and the truth about the [Uchiha Clan Downfall](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Uchiha_Clan_Downfall "Uchiha Clan Downfall"), all of which now drove Sasuke along [a path of vengeance](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Curse_of_Hatred "Curse of Hatred") against Konoha and anyone else who would dare cross his path. Naruto insisted that he could still get through to Sasuke, but Tobi laughed and left, saying that Naruto and Sasuke were fated to fight again.

![Naruto talks with Gaara about Sasuke.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/85/As_one%27s_friend.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150209080724)

Naruto talks with Gaara about Sasuke.

While Naruto was practising his Sage Mode's sensory abilities to locate Sasuke, he was interrupted by the arrival of Sakura, [Rock Lee](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Rock_Lee "Rock Lee"), Kiba and Sai. Sakura attempted to dissuade Naruto in his attempts to bring Sasuke back to Konoha by falsely telling him that she loved him. However, Naruto knew she was lying and he rejected her confession and her proposal to abandon Sasuke, stating it had nothing to do with his promise to her. After Sakura's party left, Sai's [ink clone](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ink_clone "Ink clone") revealed that the rest of the [Konoha 11](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_11 "Konoha 11") had decided to kill Sasuke themselves to prevent another war, while Sakura planned to kill Sasuke herself, at Sai's unintended insistence. [Gaara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gaara "Gaara"), who attended the [Five Kage Summit](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Five_Kage_Summit "Five Kage Summit") that Sasuke attacked, arrived and revealed Tobi's declaration of the [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"), telling Naruto how they would be fighting Sasuke to protect Naruto, but he did advise Naruto to consider for himself what was the right thing to do. After hearing that most of his friends have turned against Sasuke, who now wanted to unleash his vengeance on just about everyone, Naruto hyperventilated and passed out. When he awoke, he was told by Yamato that Kakashi went to stop Sakura from dealing with Sasuke.

![Naruto and Sasuke clash.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/f/f7/Naruto_vs._Sasuke.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222112017)

Naruto and Sasuke clash.

Using a shadow clone as a decoy, Naruto escaped from the inn and followed Kakashi, arriving just in time to save Sakura from being killed by Sasuke. He tried once more to reason with Sasuke, sympathising with his pain and acknowledging Itachi's sacrifice. However, Sasuke was unmoved and declared his resolve to destroy Konoha and sever the Uchiha's connection to the shinobi world. Naruto then clashed his Rasengan with Sasuke's Chidori, realising that everything in his life he could have easily gone down the same path of Sasuke. Despite his jealousy of him, he had come to like Sasuke and was glad to have met him. Undeterred, Sasuke gave Naruto two options: kill or be killed; Naruto chose neither. Tobi and [Zetsu](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Zetsu "Zetsu") arrived and prepared to depart with Sasuke. Naruto resolved that if he and Sasuke were to battle again, they would kill each other, but he was willing to accept it as he would shoulder Sasuke's hatred alone. Sasuke vowed to kill Naruto first and left with Tobi and Zetsu, while Naruto and his teammates returned to Konoha, with the captive [Karin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Karin "Karin"). Naruto explained the situation to his friends and asked to fight Sasuke alone, determined to get stronger for their upcoming battle.

### Power

Main article: [Power (Arc)](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Power_\(Arc\) "Power (Arc)")

### Paradise Life on a Boat

Main article: [Paradise Life on a Boat](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Paradise_Life_on_a_Boat "Paradise Life on a Boat")

### Fourth Shinobi World War: Countdown

Main article: [Fourth Shinobi World War: Countdown](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War:_Countdown "Fourth Shinobi World War: Countdown")

![Naruto meets with the Great Toad Sage.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/a2/The_Great_Toad%27s_Prediction.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150506011736)

Naruto meets with the Great Toad Sage.

As Naruto was about to eat at Ichiraku, he was suddenly summoned back to Mount Myōboku by [Fukasaku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fukasaku "Fukasaku"). He learns from the [Great Toad Sage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Toad_Sage "Great Toad Sage") 's fortune that he would meet an " [octopus](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gy%C5%ABki "Gyūki") " and would battle a " [young man with powerful eyes](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Uchiha "Sasuke Uchiha")." When [Gerotora](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gerotora "Gerotora") was summoned to give Naruto the "key" to the [Eight Trigrams Seal](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight_Trigrams_Seal "Eight Trigrams Seal"), Naruto knew he would need the Nine-Tails' power for the battles to come and accepted the key. Naruto was sent back to Ichiraku, where some of the villagers asked for his autograph. Naruto was unaware that the five Kage were planning to keep him from participating in the upcoming [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"). For his safety, Tsunade gave Naruto an "S-rank mission" on a [remote island](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genbu "Genbu") in the [Land of Lightning](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Lightning "Land of Lightning") with [Yamato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Yamato "Yamato"), [Might Guy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Might_Guy "Might Guy"), [Aoba Yamashiro](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Aoba_Yamashiro "Aoba Yamashiro"), and other Konoha-nin as security.

Once at the island, Naruto met [Killer B](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Killer_B "Killer B"), the jinchūriki of [Eight-Tails](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Eight-Tails "Eight-Tails"), and requested to train under him after marvelling at B's mastery of his tailed beast. B refused as he was on vacation, despite Naruto's best efforts to impress him. However, when [Motoi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Motoi "Motoi") heard Naruto bumped fists with B, he took Naruto was taken to [Falls of Truth](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Falls_of_Truth "Falls of Truth"), where B trained to control the Eight-Tails. Following Motoi's instructions, Naruto sat on the platform in front of the waterfall and closed his eyes to see his true self: Dark Naruto, who berated Naruto on how quickly the Konoha villagers changed their opinions of him and exclaimed that the Nine-Tails liked him better. Inside his mind, Naruto fought his dark self, but found that they were evenly matched. Breaking out of meditation, Naruto questioned Motoi about B's history in order to learn how to conquer his inner darkness.

![Naruto fights the Nine-Tails.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/4/49/Cho-odama.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20210805005445)

Naruto fights the Nine-Tails.

Naruto reminisced about how B and Gaara changed everyone's opinions about them and began to doubt if the Konoha villagers sincerely trusted him. After witnessing B save Motoi from a [giant squid](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Giant_squid "Giant squid") and resume their friendship, an inspired Naruto returned to the Falls of Truth and confronted Dark Naruto again. With Naruto now having faith in himself, Dark Naruto began to weaken as he asked what his reason for existing was. Naruto answered by hugging him, accepted Dark Naruto as a part of him while thanking him for pushing him to become a better person. Dark Naruto finally relented and faded. B led Naruto and Yamato to a special room in the [secret temple](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Temple "Tailed Beast Temple") behind the waterfall, where Naruto was prepared to fight the Nine-Tails for its chakra. Within his subconscious, Naruto unlocked the seal and engaged the Nine-Tails in battle using [Sage Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sage_Mode "Sage Mode"). Though he appeared to have the upper hand and drain the Nine-Tails' chakra, the fox instantly planted its own hatred within its absorbed chakra, consuming Naruto.

![Naruto meets his mother.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/ab/Naruto_and_Kushina_meet.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20141024185050)

Naruto meets his mother.

Just before Naruto was completely consumed, the spirit of his mother, [Kushina Uzumaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kushina_Uzumaki "Kushina Uzumaki"), appeared. Naruto believed that Kushina was the Nine-Tails in disguise, earning a hit on the head and an immediate apology from her, who hoped that he did not inherit her short-temper. Realising Kushina was his mother, he tearfully hugged her and the love-filled reunion purged the Nine-Tails' hatred within its drained chakra. With renewed confidence from hearing the story of his parents, Naruto battled the Nine-Tails once more. With his mother's assistance, Naruto successfully weakens the fox long enough to separate it from its chakra. Attaining the [Nine-Tails Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails_Chakra_Mode "Nine-Tails Chakra Mode"), Naruto imprisons the emaciated and infuriated Nine-Tails within a [new, stronger seal](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Torii_Seal "Torii Seal"), apologising to it as it faded into darkness. Afterwards, Naruto learned from Kushina about his [heritage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Uzumaki_Clan "Uzumaki Clan"), the truth behind the [Nine-Tails' attack on Konoha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tailed_Demon_Fox%27s_Attack "Nine-Tailed Demon Fox's Attack"), and how his parents gave their lives to protect him. Naruto told his mother that he could finally understand what a parent's love felt like and that he didn't blame them for what happened, instead feeling glad to be their son. As she fades away, Kushina tearfully hugged Naruto, thanking him for letting her and Minato be his parents.

![Naruto attacks Kisame.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/b/b6/Naruto_crashes_into_Kisame.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120218213914)

Naruto attacks Kisame.

In the real world, Naruto demonstrated his Nine-Tails Chakra Mode to B and Yamato before sensing [Kisame Hoshigaki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kisame_Hoshigaki "Kisame Hoshigaki") of [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki") hidden inside his [Samehada](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Samehada "Samehada") via [his negative emotions](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Negative_Emotions_Sensing "Negative Emotions Sensing"). When Kisame attempted to escape, Naruto used his blinding speed to quickly smash Kisame into the wall, though his foot gets stuck. Yamato helps Naruto out while B pursues Kisame and they regroup to see Kisame's defeat by Guy. Kisame is restrained for interrogation, though he breaks free of his confinements through sheer will and summons sharks inside a [water prison](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Water_prison "Water prison") to eat him alive. Shocked by Kisame's suicide, Naruto observes that even those in Akatsuki fight for their comrades. They examine the intel that Kisame was trying to send, which turns out to be booby-trapped: they are each caught in a water prison and are trapped alongside a shark. Another shark, meanwhile, is able to escape with Kisame's intel. After they escape, Naruto is tasked with evacuating the giant animals into the [Island Turtle](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Island_Turtle "Island Turtle") 's shell and logging the island's ecology as part of his "official" [S-rank](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/S-rank "S-rank") mission. He remains unaware that it is a ploy to keep him away from the war.

### Fourth Shinobi World War: Confrontation

Main article: [Fourth Shinobi World War: Confrontation](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War:_Confrontation "Fourth Shinobi World War: Confrontation")

![Naruto senses the war with Sage Mode.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/6/6e/Naruto_senses_the_war.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20230207041812)

Naruto senses the war with Sage Mode.

Naruto returned to the Falls of Truth with B to start practising his [Nine-Tails Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nine-Tails_Chakra_Mode "Nine-Tails Chakra Mode"), specifically learning how to use [Tailed Beast Balls](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Ball "Tailed Beast Ball"). Since Tailed Beast Balls couldn't be made without the Nine-Tails' cooperation, Naruto tried to create the [Tailed Beast Rasengan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tailed_Beast_Rasengan "Tailed Beast Rasengan") as a workaround. While struggling to balance the jutsu's composition, he sensed a [distant source](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kinkaku "Kinkaku") of the Nine-Tails' chakra. Naruto left the Falls of Truth to investigate and was met by a contingent of Konoha-nin, amongst them [Iruka Umino](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Iruka_Umino "Iruka Umino"). Iruka tried to convince him to go back to his training, but Naruto bypassed them and, from entering Sage Mode, sensed the ongoing [Fourth Shinobi World War](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Shinobi_World_War "Fourth Shinobi World War"). Naruto became angry that they would try to keep the war a secret from him and that they wouldn't let him help. Iruka apologised, yet still made an effort to [restrain](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/String_Light_Formation "String Light Formation") him. Naruto escaped and found a [Self-Repairing Barrier](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Self-Repairing_Barrier "Self-Repairing Barrier"), preventing him from leaving by himself. B, at Iruka's request, decided to join him, and together they broke through the barrier.

![Naruto states his resolve to the Nine-Tails.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/d/de/Naruto_in_Kyubi%27s_eye.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20250520193402)

Naruto states his resolve to the Nine-Tails.

Shortly after, the Nine-Tails pulled Naruto into his subconscious, berating him for squandering its chakra and calling him naive for thinking he could stop the war by himself. When the Nine-Tails failed to once again tempt Naruto with power, the fox went off to claim that his attempt to put an end to hatred was futile, using his history with Sasuke as proof. Naruto responded by [pinning](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Torii_Seal "Torii Seal") the fox down, refuting that it was the one who was being naive and confidently exclaiming he would find a way to deal with Sasuke and end the war. Before he leaves, Naruto also promises to resolve the Nine-Tails' own hatred someday, unnerving the fox.

![Naruto outpaces A.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/a/a5/Naruto_bypasses_A.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150214203904)

Naruto outpaces A.

On their way to the battlefield, Naruto and B were met by the [Fourth Raikage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Fourth_Raikage "Fourth Raikage") and [Tsunade](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Tsunade "Tsunade"), both intent on stopping the jinchūriki. B tried to convince the Raikage to let them go, and when that failed, Naruto tried to get around them, but the Raikage's [Lightning Release Chakra Mode](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Lightning_Release_Chakra_Mode "Lightning Release Chakra Mode") was too fast for that. The Raikage became increasingly aggressive in his determination to stop Naruto and B from joining the war effort, going so far as to threaten to kill Naruto if it would keep Akatsuki from capturing the Nine-Tails. First B and then Tsunade joined Naruto in arguing to allow them to fight, which the Raikage relented to once Naruto dodges his maximum speed. On Tsunade's orders, [Shikaku Nara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shikaku_Nara "Shikaku Nara") contacted Naruto and informed him about Akatsuki's [White Zetsu Army](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/White_Zetsu_Army "White Zetsu Army") and the accompanying [Impure World Reincarnations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Impure_World_Reincarnation "Impure World Reincarnation"). Naruto and B soon afterwards encountered a squad of seeming Allied forces, but with his Nine-Tails Chakra Mode's [ability](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Negative_Emotions_Sensing "Negative Emotions Sensing"), it allowed him to detect them as disguised Zetsu. He quickly defeated them and sent shadow clones to various other battlefields to lend assistance.

![Naruto fights Nagato.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/f/f9/Naruto_kills_dog.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150222111845)

Naruto fights Nagato.
