## Creation and Conception

When creating Naruto, [Masashi Kishimoto](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Masashi_Kishimoto "Masashi Kishimoto") incorporated into the character a number of traits he felt made an ideal hero: a straightforward way of thinking, a mischievous side, and many of the attributes possessed by [Son Gokū](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goku "wikipedia:Goku") from the *[Dragon Ball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dragon_Ball "wikipedia:Dragon Ball")* franchise. He also made sure to keep Naruto "simple and stupid," since he does not like smart characters. Naruto himself is not modeled after anyone in particular, being conceived as childlike, with something of a dark side as a result of his harsh past. Despite this, he is always positive, making him unique in Kishimoto's eyes.

![The first version of Naruto that Masashi Kishimoto drew.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/8/8b/FIRST_NARUTO.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20100227203135)

The first version of Naruto that Masashi Kishimoto drew.

Naruto's wardrobe is based on clothing Kishimoto wore when he was younger; according to Kishimoto, using a pre-existing design would not have made Naruto unique, whereas something original would have made him stand out too much. The orange colouring of his costume is used to make Naruto "pop," with blues often being used to complement the orange. Because Naruto is associated with spirals, swirl patterns are incorporated into his costume. Initial illustrations of Naruto had him wearing boots, but Kishimoto substituted these for sandals, because he likes drawing toes. The goggles that Naruto used to wear were also replaced with a forehead protector, because the goggles themselves were too time-consuming to draw. Kishimoto said that he felt glad that his character had blond hair and blue eyes. The editor of *Shōnen Jump* in the United States added that Kishimoto implied that the traits may have led the character to appeal to a Western audience. Kishimoto said that he most identifies with Naruto out of all of the *Naruto* characters (in an interview with the U.S. *Shōnen Jump*, Kishimoto stated that his childhood was like that of Naruto's years while he was enrolled in the Academy).

![Naruto's appearances throughout the series.](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/4/4b/Naruto_Appearances.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20150630074159)

Naruto's appearances throughout the series.

In the Japanese versions of *Naruto*, Naruto often ends his sentences with the addendum " [\-ttebayo](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Catchphrases_and_Verbal_Tics#Dattebayo.2C_Dattebane_and_Dattebasa "Catchphrases and Verbal Tics") " (which gives a similar effect to ending a sentence with "you know?"). Kishimoto wanted to give Naruto a childlike catchphrase, and "dattebayo" came to mind. It complemented Naruto's character, and served as a verbal tic that showed him to be something of a brat. Throughout the beginning of the English [anime](https://animanga.fandom.com/wiki/anime "w:c:animanga:anime"), the dub replaced "dattebayo" and "-ttebayo" with the phrase "believe it!" to mirror the effect, as well as to match the character's lip movements.

## Trivia

| ![Springtime of Youth](https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/naruto/images/3/39/Youth_symbol.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/40?cb=20130120020002)  Springtime of Youth | The [*Springtime of Youth* wiki](https://springtimeofyouth.fandom.com/wiki/ "w:c:springtimeofyouth") has an article about this topic:  **[Naruto Uzumaki](https://springtimeofyouth.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Uzumaki "w:c:springtimeofyouth:Naruto Uzumaki")** |
| --- | --- |

- Spiral patterns are a recurring theme in *Naruto*, especially in relation to the character of Naruto Uzumaki himself. The name "Naruto" is short for " [narutomaki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Narutomaki "wikipedia:Narutomaki") " (なると巻き), a kind of [kamaboko](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/kamaboko "wikipedia:kamaboko") with a pink spiral design in the middle that is used as a topping for ramen, which is Naruto's favourite food. His family name "Uzumaki" can mean " [whirlpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whirlpool "wikipedia:Whirlpool") " or " [maelstrom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maelstrom "wikipedia:Maelstrom")." His full name is also a reference to the [Naruto whirlpools](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naruto_whirlpools "wikipedia:Naruto whirlpools") (鳴門の渦潮, *Naruto no uzushio*), named after the [city in the Tokushima Prefecture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naruto,_Tokushima "wikipedia:Naruto, Tokushima") in Japan. In addition, Naruto's mother, Kushina Uzumaki, is a relative to the [Uzumaki clan](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Uzumaki_clan "Uzumaki clan") from [Uzushiogakure](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Uzushiogakure "Uzushiogakure") in the [Land of Whirlpools](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Land_of_Whirlpools "Land of Whirlpools"), who used the spiral pattern as both their clan and village symbol.
- According to the databook(s):
	- Naruto's hobbies are pulling pranks and watering plants.
		- Naruto wishes to fight [Sasuke Uchiha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke_Uchiha "Sasuke Uchiha"), the [Third Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Third_Hokage "Third Hokage"),[^1] and members of [Akatsuki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Akatsuki "Akatsuki").[^2] [^4]
		- Naruto's favourite foods are ramen from [Ramen Ichiraku](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ramen_Ichiraku "Ramen Ichiraku") and [oshiruko](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/red_bean_soup "wikipedia:red bean soup"). His least favourite are fresh [vegetables](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/vegetable "wikipedia:vegetable"), which others, especially Kakashi (barring Hinata) have attempted to make him eat with little success. Outside the databook, he's known to enjoy ramen in general, often seen eating cup noodles.
		- Naruto has completed 16 official missions in total: 7 D-rank, 1 C-rank, 2 B-rank, 6 A-rank, 0 S-rank.
		- Naruto's favourite phrase is "a large serving of [miso](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/miso "wikipedia:miso") ramen with [roasted pork fillet](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Char_siu "wikipedia:Char siu") " (味噌チャーシュー大盛り, *miso chāshū ōmori*).
- According to an early anime profile, Naruto's potential is immeasurable (計り知れない, *hakarishirenai*) due hosting the Nine-Tailed Fox.[^169]
- According to character trivia from *[Boruto: Naruto Next Generations](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boruto:_Naruto_Next_Generations "Boruto: Naruto Next Generations")*:
	- His attributes are: 120 in perception, 160 in dexterity, 170 in negotiations, 110 in strength, 90 in intelligence, and unknown in chakra.
		- He was also given a skill rating in these particular areas:
		- Evasion: ★★★★★
				- Unarmed Hand-to-hand Fighting: ★★★★☆
				- Manners: ★★☆☆☆
- According to [Hyō no Sho](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hy%C5%8D_no_Sho "Hyō no Sho"), while in the [Academy](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Academy "Academy"), Naruto had an A in positivity, a B taijutsu, and an F in ninjutsu, genjutsu, cooperation, and classroom attitude.
- October 10, Naruto's birthday, was [Health and Sports Day](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sports_Day_\(Japan\) "wikipedia:Sports Day (Japan)") in Japan when his character was conceived. The holiday, however, was later moved to the second Monday of October in 1999.
- Naruto consistently ranked in the top two (taking first place twice) in the first five official *Shōnen Jump* character popularity polls. However, in the sixth character poll, he was ranked fourth. According to the third databook character poll, which adds up all the votes from the first six polls, Naruto took second place. In the most recent poll, Naruto regained his status and reached first.
	- Naruto is one of four characters to have always stayed in the top ten of every character popularity poll.
- Naruto is the fourth known jinchūriki known to have survived the extraction of a tailed beast, along with Hagoromo Ōtsutsuki, Kushina Uzumaki, and Obito Uchiha.[^80]
- Naruto's mother was nicknamed the "Red Hot-Blooded Habanero" while his father was famed as the "Yellow Flash." Naruto's appropriate self-styled title of " **Konoha's Orange Hokage** " refers to the fact that the colour orange is a mixture of red and yellow.
- When Naruto first became a genin, while using his [Sexy Technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sexy_Technique "Sexy Technique"), his settei reveals that he is 160cm tall.
- Kishimoto's various concept art of Naruto for *The Last: Naruto the Movie* showed that his height had gone through various changes: first 170cm, then 173cm, and finally 180cm.
- Despite Naruto being a user of the Truth-Seeking Balls and gaining Hagoromo's power, both of which contain Yin-Yang Release, Yin Release is not listed as one of his natures in the fourth databook.[^5]
- [Eiichiro Oda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eiichiro_Oda "wikipedia:Eiichiro Oda"), author of *[One Piece](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/One_Piece_\(Manga\) "w:c:onepiece:One Piece (Manga)")*, stated that his character [Sanji](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Sanji "w:c:onepiece:Sanji") 's name was originally going to be Naruto, but changed it because the *Naruto* series was about to become serialised and wanted to avoid confusion.
	- Naruto makes an undetailed background appearance on the cover page of the [766th chapter](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Chapter_766 "w:c:onepiece:Chapter 766") of *One Piece*, where the person behind [Nami](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Nami "w:c:onepiece:Nami") (who is wearing a changpao with the Konoha symbol) is implied to be Naruto. Also, both main characters are seen eating each others' respective favourite foods, as Naruto is eating meat, and [Monkey D. Luffy](https://onepiece.fandom.com/wiki/Monkey_D._Luffy "w:c:onepiece:Monkey D. Luffy") is eating ramen. Additionally, this chapter was released on the same week as the release of the [final](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Seal_of_Reconciliation "The Seal of Reconciliation") [two](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Naruto_Uzumaki!!_\(chapter_700\) "Naruto Uzumaki!! (chapter 700)") *Naruto* manga chapters.
		- In the 700th chapter of the *Naruto* manga, the symbol of the Straw Hat Pirates appears on the forehead protector of Naruto's statue as part of [Boruto](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boruto "Boruto") 's graffiti.
- While Naruto was initially the shortest of the [Konoha 11](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha_11 "Konoha 11"), he ultimately becomes second tallest, after Shino.
- In the original manga pilot, Naruto was actually a demon fox and lived his life as a human boy, and the Nine-Tailed Fox that attacked the village was his father.
- Although he was never raised by Minato, Naruto seemingly inherited his father's fighting style of almost never relying on hand seals, with the exception of his [signature technique](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Shadow_Clone_Technique "Shadow Clone Technique").
- In the anime, Naruto is a lightweight drinker.[^170]

## Quotes

*See also: [Dattebayo and Believe It](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Dattebayo_and_Believe_It "Dattebayo and Believe It")*

- (To [Kakashi](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kakashi "Kakashi")) " *And my future dream is to be the greatest [Hokage](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hokage "Hokage")! Then the whole village will stop disrespecting me and start treating me like I'm somebody. Somebody important!*" [^171]
- (To [Ibiki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Ibiki "Ibiki")) " *Don't underestimate me! I don't quit and I don't run! You can act tough all you want! You're not gonna scare me off! No way! I don't care if I do get stuck as a [genin](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Genin "Genin") for the rest of my life! I'll still be Hokage someday!*" [^172]
- (To [Neji](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Neji "Neji")) " *I'm not gonna run away, I never go back on my word! That's my **[nindō](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nind%C5%8D "Nindō")**: my ninja way!*" [^173]
- (To [Gaara](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Gaara "Gaara")) " *It's almost unbearable, isn't it… the pain of being all alone. I know that feeling; I've been there, in that dark and lonely place, but now there are others, other people who mean a lot to me. I care more about them than I do myself, and I won't let anyone hurt them. That's why I'll never give up. I will stop you, even if I have to kill you! They saved me from myself. They rescued me from my loneliness. They were the first to accept me as who I am. They're my friends.*" [^174]
- (To [Pain](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Pain "Pain")) " *Just give up… on trying to make me give up!*" [^175]
- (To [Nagato](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Nagato "Nagato"), reciting a line from [Jiraiya](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jiraiya "Jiraiya") 's [first book](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/The_Tale_of_the_Utterly_Gutsy_Shinobi "The Tale of the Utterly Gutsy Shinobi")) " *Then I will break that curse. If there's such thing as peace, I will find it. I won't give up!*" [^41]
- (To [Sasuke](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Sasuke "Sasuke")) " *If you attack [Konoha](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konoha "Konoha"), I will have to fight you… So save up your hatred and take it all on me, I'm the only one who can take it! It's the only thing I can do! I will shoulder your hatred and die with you! Because I'm your friend!*" [^176]
- (To [his mother](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kushina "Kushina")) " *Don't apologise… I had a lot of hard times growing up as a [jinchūriki](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Jinch%C5%ABriki "Jinchūriki"), but I never blamed you or [Dad](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Minato_Namikaze "Minato Namikaze"). I couldn't understand what a parent's love was like because you guys were never there… so I could only guess… But now I know… I live because you and Dad gave your lives for me and filled me up with love before the Nine-Tails was inside me! So here I am, happy and healthy! I'm glad I ended up being your son!*" [^177]
- (To [Kurama](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Kurama "Kurama")) " *You're not the monster fox anymore. You're one of my teammates from Konoha… **Kurama.***" [^178]
- (To [Obito](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Obito "Obito")) " *Quit it with your stupid reasoning! I meant to say that I'll stand any pain for my friends!! I'm not gonna give up on them!! It might just be me being selfish, but… not having my friends here… is the most painful thing for me!! **Period!!!!***" [^179]
- (About [Hinata](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Hinata "Hinata")) " *Hinata… This big idiot finally understands… The real meaning of the scarf you gave to me when you went with [Toneri](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Toneri "Toneri") … I know now, from the scarf you painstakingly knitted and took so long to finish, that your love can't be unravelled so easily… Wait for me… This whole time, you always loved me for the way I am… Now, as a man, there's something I must tell you… Hinata, I swear I **will** save you!*" [^23]
- (To Hinata) " *Hinata… Do you remember that lesson in class? The one with, "If the end of the world came, who would you spend your last day with?" I couldn't write anyone's name down. I didn't know my parents, and I didn't have any friends… But now, I know exactly whom I'd choose. I want to be with you. Now and forever, until I die… I want to be with you, Hinata!*" [^23]
- (To [his son](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Boruto "Boruto")) " *Boruto… with what I do now, all the people in this village are like a family. So there will be times that you won't be able to have me as a father all to yourself. I know this must be hard for you… but you must learn to endure it. You're a shinobi too, aren't you?*" [^180]
- (To [the village](https://naruto.fandom.com/wiki/Konohagakure "Konohagakure")) " *However, I believe there are many who are dissatisfied. This village is still undergoing development. And during all this, there are those who are facing hard and demanding times. But I don't agree with that. Changing the things before us, a little by little, even one thing at a time, will make the world a better place. I want Konohagakure to be a place where people smile and enjoy their lives. Will you lend me your strength? Let's change Konohagakure together!*" [^181]
